1b1(phal chen): @##//'phags pa sangs rgyas 
1b2(phal chen): phal po che zhes bya ba/bam po 
1b3(phal chen): drug cu rtsa gsum pa/de nas byang chub 
2a1(phal chen): @##//sems dpa'i tshogs chen po de'i nang nas/byang chub 
2a2(phal chen): sems dpa' kha cig 'di snyam du sems te/sangs rgyas bcom 
2a3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing ni ji tsam zhig tu bsam 
2a4(phal chen): gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang /
3a1(phal chen): @#//gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
3a2(phal chen): gsung ni ji tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes 
3a3(phal chen): ni ji tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs
3a4(phal chen): pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa ni ji tsam zhig tu bsam gyas mi khyab/sangs rgyas sa bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi 
3a5(phal chen): chags pa mi mnga' ba'i rnam par spyod pa ni ja tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi khyab/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
3b1(phal chen): rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa ni ji tsam zhig tu bsam gyis mi khyab snyam pa dang /de nas bcom ldan 'das 
3b2(phal chen): kyis byang chub sems dpa' de dag gi sems thugs kyis thugs su chud nas/byang chub sems dpa' ud pa la'i snying 
3b3(phal chen): po|sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis byin tu brlabaso//sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyi byin dang ldan par 
3b4(phal chen): mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'od kyis snang bar mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi gzi brjid kyis 
3b5(phal chen): yongasu rgyas par mdzad do//sangs rgyasakyi mi 'jig pa la rab tu bzhag go/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa 
3b6(phal chen): khong du chud par mdzad do//sangs rgya kyi bdag nyid che ba nyid dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi yul rab tu rtogs par mdzad do//chags 
4a1(phal chen): @#//pa med pa'i rgyud 'byam klas pa la rab tu bzhag go/sangs rgyas thams cad gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa'i rigs rab tu rtogs 
4a2(phal chen): par mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi tshul gyi gnas brjod du med pa la rab tu bzhag pa mdzad do//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ud+pa la'i snying pos chos kyi dbyings 
4a3(phal chen): la 'jug pa rig cing khong du chud pas/chags pa med pa'i dbyings su bsgrubs pa'i yid dang /chos thams cad la bsgribs pa med par gnas pa'i byang chub sems 
4a4(phal chen): dpa'i spyod pa dang /kun tu bzang po'i smon lam dang ldan pa'i yang dag par 'jug pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjes su song ba'i sangs rgyas 
4a5(phal chen): blta ba dang /snying rje chen pos lta ba'i sems can thams cad yongs su sbyongs ba'i go cha chen po dang /byang chub sems dpa'i yi dam dang /
4a6(phal chen): spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor rgyun chad pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes mngon par sgrub pa skad cig gi cha shas la rnam par spyod pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid 
4a7(phal chen): kyi sgo mthong ba mi zad pa dang /tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa'i rnam pa/gzungs thams cad kyis snang bar byas pa dang ldan pas sangs rgyas kyi mthus byang chub 
4a8(phal chen): sems dpa' padamo'i snying po la 'di skad ces smraso//kye rgyal ba'i sras sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni tshad med pa la rnam par spyod pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras 
4b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni rnam par 'phrul pa chen po la rnam par spyod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa thams cad la sku'i mtshan nyid thams cad 
4b2(phal chen): rab tu brnyes pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams kyi chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba ni ting nge 'dzin dang dgongs pas sbyar ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa 
4b3(phal chen): bzhi ni brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa shin tu ston pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
4b4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi gsung dang dbyangs ni tshad med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings thugs su chud pa ni brjod du med pa'o//sangs rgyas 
4b5(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgas kyi khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba ni/thams cad mngon par mkhyen pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i srasadag sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyis chos kyi dbyings thugs suchud cing 
4b6(phal chen): bstan pa ni bsgribs pa med pa'i mthar thug pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sra sa bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis chos kyi dbyigs mtha' dang dbung med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnam pa bcu ste/bcu gang zhe na/
4b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi gzugs kyi sku rnam par dag pa ni rnyog pa med par rgyud thaMd du byung bas mtha' dang dbung med ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'i rnams kyi chags pa'i spyan ni/chos thaMd mnyam pa nyid 
4b8(phal chen): kyi spyan mngon par sangs rgyas pas mtha' dang dbung med ma'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi chags pa med pa'i snyan ni/'gro ba thaMd kyi sgra bsgribs pa med par thugs su chud pas mtha' dang dbung me dpa'o//sangs rgyas 
5a1(phal chen): @#//bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shangs kyi skye mched ni/sangs rgyas kyi mnga' thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pas mtha' dang dbung med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis ljags shin tu yangs pa 
5a2(phal chen): ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad du gsung gi dbyangs rjes su 'gro bas mtha' dang dbung med ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldas rnams kyi sku'i chos nyid ni/sems can thams cad ma lus pa'i sems kyi rnam pa de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba rnam par dmigs pas 
5a3(phal chen): mtha' dang dbung med pa 'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi thugs kyi phrin las ni/chos kyi sku dang tha mi dad cing /dus gsum chags pa med par thugs su chud cing rnam par dag pas mtha' dang dbung me dpa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
5a4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par thar pa bsgribs pa mi mnga' ba la rnam par spyod pa ni sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang dang /rnam 'phrul ba dang /cho 'phrul mi zad pa yang dag par ston pas mtha' dang dbung med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 
5a5(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rnam par dag pa ni/brjod du med pa'i rgyan byin gyis brlabs pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing nye bar nyon mongs pa med pa yang dag par bstan te/bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dmigs par mdzad pas 
5a6(phal chen): mtha dang dbung med pa'o//sad+yas bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i khyad par dang /smon lam thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa ni/rnam par rol pa'i ye shes kyi chos yongs su rdzogs te/chos thams cad rdzogs par byang chub par bya ba la mtha' dang dbung 
5a7(phal chen): med pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbung med ba'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po' di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
5a8(phal chen): kyi sangs rgyas kyi skad cig gcig la ye shes kyis mngon bsgrubs pa yang dag par bstan pa'o//bcu gang zhe na/skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du dga' ldan gyi gnas nas 'pho ba yongs su bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 
5b1(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thaMd du byang chub sems dpa'i tshe yongasu bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thaMd mngon par 'byung ba yongasu bstan pa dang /skang cig gcig la 'jig rten gi khams thams cad du byang 
5b2(phal chen): chub kyi shing drung du bzhugs shing mngon par 'tshang brgya ba yongasu bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig+te gyi khams thams cad du chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba yongasu bstan pa dang //skad cig gcig la 'jig rjen gyi kham+sa
5b3(phal chen): thaMd du 'dul ba'i sems can yongasu smin pa'i tshul ji lta ba bzhin du dus la bab par shes pas/sang+yas bsnyen bskur ba yongasu bstan pa dang/skad cig gcig la 'jigarten gyi khams thams cad seMn ji ltar 'dul ba'i tshul 
5b4(phal chen): bzhin du de bzhin gsheDa pa brjod du med pa dang /sang+yas kyi sku sna tshogs rnam par dmigs pa yongasu bstan pa dang //skad cig gcig la 'jigasten khams thaMd du rgyan sna tshogs dang /rgyan brjod du med pas de bzhin gshegs 
5b5(phal chen): pa'i khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba dang /thaMd mkhyen pa'i mdzod yongasu bstan pa dang /skad cig gcig la 'jig rten gyi khams thaMd du rnam par dag pa gcig ma yin pa thaMd yongasu bstan pa dang /
5b6(phal chen): skad cig gcig la 'jig sten gyi khams thaMd du 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sang+yas skye ba thaMd du dbang po dang /brtson 'grus rnam pa tha dad byung ba dang /mos pa dang /spyod pa rnam pa tha dad pa byung ba la dus gsum gyi sang+yas kyi rgyud 
5b7(phal chen): du mngon par 'tshang rgya ba yongasu bstan pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bac+doMs rnams kyi sang+yas kyi skad cig la yais mngon par sgrub pa yang dag par bstan pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu pa 'di dag ni/
5b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi dus las mi 'da' ste /bcu gang zhe na//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams mngon par 'tshang rgya ba ni de'i dus las mi 
6a1(phal chen): @#//'da' bas dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi dge ba rnam par smin pa dang /rnam par ma smin pa mnyam pa nyid ni de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus 
6a2(phal chen): la bab pa yin/sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams kyis yongs su smin pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams lung bstan pa ni/de'i dus las mi 'da' ba dus la bab pa yin/sadas rgyas bcom ldan 
6a3(phal chen): 'das rnams kyis sems can gyi bsam pa'i rgyud du cho 'phrul yongs su bstan pa ni de'i dus las mi 'da'abs dus la bab pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyis sems can mos pa ji lta bu dang ldan 
6a4(phal chen): pa'i tshul bzhin du sku yongs su bstan pa ni dus las mi 'da' bas ngus la bab pa yin/sangs d+yas bcom ldan 'das rnams/btang snyoms su bzhugs pa ni/de'i dus las mi gda' bas dus 
6a5(phal chen): la bab payin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams yul dang grong dang grong rdal du gshegs pa ni/de'i dus la mi 'da' bas dus la bab pa yin no//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
6a6(phal chen): 'dasarnams dad pa ji lta bu dang ldan pa'i tshul bzhin du sems can yongs su 'dzin pa ni/de'i dus las ma 'da' bas dus la bab yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams sems can kun nas nyon mongs pa 
6a7(phal chen): dang ldan zhing gdul dka' ba la gzhan la ltos pa mi mnga' bar 'dul ba ni de'i dus las mi 'da' bas dus la babs pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa bsam gyis mi 
6a8(phal chen): khyab pas rnam par 'phrul pa bstan pa de'i dus las mi 'da' pas dus la bab pa yin/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
6b1(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi yul dpe med cing bsam gyis mi khyab ste/bcu gang zhe na/'jig rten gyis khams thams cad ma lus par dkyil 
6b2(phal chen): mo dkrung gcig gis rgyas par dgang ba dang /dus gcig tu sangs rgyas thams cad yongs su bstan pa dang /'od zer gcig gis 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad snang bar bya ba dang /sku gzugs gcig 
6b3(phal chen): gi lus dang /gzugs thams cad yongasu bstan pa dang /gnas gcig na bzhugs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ma lus bar byon pa'i skur 'gyur ba dang /ye shes gcig rtogs pas chos thams cad la 
6b4(phal chen): bsgribs pa med par 'gyur ba dang /thugs gcig bskyed pas chos kyi/dbyings thams cad ma lus par rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi che ba nyid tshad med pa dang ldan ba'i gshegs stabs 
6b5(phal chen): gcig gis de kho na nyid yang dag par bstan pa dang /dus gsum thams cad sangs rgyas kyi go 'phang gis rgyas par 'geng sa pa dang /'gro ba thams cad dang ma 'dres bar bzhugs shing /
6b6(phal chen): 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang mi dmigs pa'i tshul gyis rgyud kyi tshul gyi gcig pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
6b7(phal chen): rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi yul dpe med cing bsam gyis mi khyab pa'o //kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes te/
6b8(phal chen): bcu gang zhe na/'jug pa med pa'i chos thams cad la 'jug pas/smon lam mngon par sgrub pa'i ye she sa dang /lus med pa la lus mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shais dang /chos thams cad mi gnyis pa 
7a1(phal chen): la mngon par 'tshang rgya ba mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes dang /sems can gyi mthar thugs par bdag med pa la sems can 'dul ba mngon par sgrub pa'i yi shes dang /chos thams 
7a2(phal chen): cad mtshan ma med pa la/mtshan ma sna tshogs mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shais dang /'jig rten gyi khams kyi yul ma skyes pa la 'jig rten gyi khams 'du ba dang /'jig pa mngon par sgrub pa'i ye she sa dang /yul thams 
7a3(phal chen): cad mngon par 'du bya ba med pa la las kyi rnam par smin pa mngon par sgrub pa'i yi shais dang /chos thams cad brjod du med pa la chos kyi dbyings su gsung mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shis dang /chos thams cadakun nas 
7a4(phal chen): nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang bas 'jug pa med pa la kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnams par byang ba mngon par sgrub pa'i shes dang /chos thams cad ma skyes pa la skye ba dang /'jig pa'i mtshan 
7a5(phal chen): nyid rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes te kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mngon par sgrub pa'i ye shes so//kye 
7a6(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshad med pa'i khongs su chud pa dag ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sku ni dus gsum thams cad kyi 
7a7(phal chen): rjes su 'brang ba'i khongs su chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams sems can thams cad 'dul bar mdzad pa ni/cho 'phrul chen po gsum gyi khongs su pa yin/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams tshig 
7a8(phal chen): rab tu dbye ba thams cad la mkhas pa ni/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi gzungs thams cad kyi khongs su chud pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi chos kyi 'khor lo thams cad ni/de bzhin gshegs 
7b1(phal chen): pa'i tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa bzhir khongasu chud pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can thams cad yongasu mi gtong ba ni/thugs rje chen po'i khongasu chud pa yin/sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das
7b2(phal chen): rnams rtag tu mnyam par bzhag pa ni/dus thams cad du sems can thams cad la rnam par gzigs pa'i khongs su chud pa yin/sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba ni/sems
7b3(phal chen): can rnam par gdul ba thams cad khongasu chud pa yin/sangasargyus bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi chags pa mi mnga' bar spyod pa ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad khongs su chud pa yin//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'
7b4(phal chen): rnams kyi skad cig gcig tu ni 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas skye ba thams cad khongs su chudapa yin/sangs rgyas bcom lan 'das rnams kyi nyi ma gcig tu ni/dus
7b5(phal chen): gsum gyi bskal pa'i grangs khongasu chud par bstan pa thams cad khongs su chud yin/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams gyi tshad med pa'i
7b6(phal chen): khongasu chud pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par bka' ba rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/bdud kyi dkrugs ma rab
7b7(phal chen): tu 'joms zhing rnam par 'thor ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi shin tu rgya tshe ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//mu stegs can gzhan dang /phas kyi rgol ba tshar bcad pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das
7b8(phal chen): rnams kyi shin tu rgya che bas chos bzod par dka' pa'o//'dul ba'i sems can thams cad rab tu tshim par mdzad pa ni/sangs rgyas bcomaldana'adas rnams kyi shin turgya che ba'i chos bzod pa dka' ba'o//sems
8a1(phal chen): @#//can 'dul ba'i dbang gis 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad gshegs pa ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//chos kyi dbyings thugs 
8a2(phal chen): su chud pa'i ye shes ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba chos bzod par dka' pa'o//'jig rten gyi khams thams chad ma lus par skus khyab cing /sangs rgyas kyi sku rnam par phye ba 
8a3(phal chen): sna tshogs thams cad du mi gnyis pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi shin tu rgya tshe ba'i chos bzod par dka'o//gsung gi yan lag thams cad la sems can mos par bya ba dang /thos par bya ba 
8a4(phal chen): ba dang /gdul bar bya ba don yod par tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa thogs pa med pas bstan pa'i tshul du tha dad pa yang dag paramakhyen pa bzhi 'byung ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan
8a5(phal chen): kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chosabazod par dka' ba'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan las rnams kyis bsdu'i bu gag cig nas/de bzhin gshegs pa 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba 
8a6(phal chen): snyed/bsam gyis mi khyab cing thogs pa med pa'i tshul du 'jig rten gyi khams thamasacad kyi rdul shin phra ba snyed kyi thabs kyis 'byung zhing /de bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi sku'i ba spu'i bu ga thams cad nas 'byung ba ni/
8a7(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dga' ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rdul shin tu phra ba gcig la sangs rgyas kyi zhing 
8a8(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thams chad kyi rdul shin tu phra bsnyed yongasu ston cing /sangs rgyas kyi rgyan dang /rang bzhin gyi lhab lhub dang /chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba rgyun mi 'chad cing /sems can rnam par 'dul ba 
8b1(phal chen): thogs pa med la 'jig rten gyi khams de dag tu gtogs pa rnams la'ang mi bde ba dang /gnod pa med kyang rdul shin tu phra ba de yang yangs par ma byas/'jig rten gyi khams de dagkyang chung bar ma byas par ji ltar gnas 
8b2(phal chen): pa'i chos kyi dbyings kyang thugs su chud pa'i ye shes mnga' ba ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya che ba'i chos bzod par dka' ba'o//mun pa med pa'i khams de bzhin gshegs pas mngon par sangs
8b3(phal chen): rgyas te/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la mun pa med cing bsgribs pa mi mnga' la/sems can pha rol gyi bsgribs pa sna tshogs kyi gnas par sel na/de la bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang 
8b4(phal chen): ldan pa ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi shin tu rgya tsho ba'i chos la bzod par dka' ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan
8b5(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi shin tu rgya tshe ba'i chos bzod par dga' ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom
8b6(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba rnam pa thams cad du/kha na ma tho ba med cing /shin tu dag pa ste/
8b7(phal chen): bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan dangs rnams kyi yang dag par bsgrubs pa bdag nyid che ba ni/sngon gyi mtha' nas rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 
8b8(phal chen): rnams kyi 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi rgyud du rigs dang ldan par 'byung ba'i bdag nyid che ba ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs rnam par dag pas/rnam pa thams cad du kha na
8b9(phal chen): med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni rnam par shes pa la mi gnas pa ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi bdag nyid tshe ba ni/phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thugs par gnas
9a1(phal chen): @#//pas/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan rnams ni dus gsum la mi gnas par rnam par spyod cing chos thams cad la rten pa 
9a2(phal chen): yang dag par bcom pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cadu kha na ma tho ba med cing shing tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni dngos po rnam 
9a3(phal chen): pa tha dad pa la rnam par gzhag pa gcig gis len pa med pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdaganyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma thob med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcoM
9a4(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mthar chos kyi dbyings la gnas pas mnyam pa'i bsod nams mi zad pa dang ldan pas/mi zad pa'i bsod nams mnga' ba ste/sangs rgyas bcom 
9a5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa mthams cad du kha na matho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni sku gzugs dang /kha dog mtha' 
9a6(phal chen): yas pas/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad na/sems can 'dul ba'i dus dang dbang gis chags pa med par bzhugs bas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdag: nyid che ba ni/rnam par thams cadu kha na 
9a7(phal chen): ma tho ba med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnam+sa ni mi 'jigs pa bzhi brnyes pas bag tsha ba mi mnga' bar bzhugs shing khyu mchog dang seng ge'i nga ro 'byin te/rgol ba'i nang na stag lta bu'i
9a8(phal chen): mthu dang ldan la/chos thams cad bstan pa la thugs sgron ma lta bu mnga' bas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bdaganyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cadu kha na ma tho ba med cing shin du rnam par dag pa'o//
9b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni yongasu mya ngan las 'das nas bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa zhig lon yang mtshan gyi tshig thos pa tsam gyis bzhugs pa dang 'dra bar sems can rnams kyi 
9b2(phal chen): yon shin tu sbyong zhing /mya ngan las 'das nas de srid kyi yun ring po 'das kyang /sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i yon zad par mi 'gyur bas/sangs rgyas kyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cad du kha na ma tho ba 
9b3(phal chen): med cing shin tu rnam par dag pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams 'jig rten gyis khams brjod du med pa'i yang rjod du med pa dag na bzhugs shing /'jig rten gyi khams rgyang ring po na bzhugs kyang /'jig 
9b4(phal chen): rten gyi khams 'di na gnas pa'i sems can rnams kyis rjesu dran par byas na/sku mngon par ston pa mdzad pas/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasrnams kyi bdag nyid che ba ni/rnam pa thams cad du khan ma tho 
9b5(phal chen): ba med cing shin tu rnam par bdag pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'as rnams kyi bdag nyid che bani/rnam pa thams cadu kha na ma tho med cing shin tu rnam par
9b6(phal chen): dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas bco ma ldan 'das rnams kyi shin tu rnam par dag pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi tshangs
9b7(phal chen): pa'i spyod pa rnam par dag pa la/sngon gyi smon lam gyis shin tu rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan'das rnams ni chags pa med pa'i tshul khrims kyis shin tu rnam par dag yin/sangs 
9b8(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/sems can thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa sel bas/shin tu rnam par dag pa ni yin/sangs rgyas bcomldana'adas rnams ni/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gis shin tu rnam par dag
10a1(phal chen): pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'khor gyis shin tu rnam par dag pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni rigs rnam par dag pas/shin tu rnam par dag pa 
10a2(phal chen): yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi phung po bsgribs pa med pas shin tu rnam par dag yin/sangs rgyas bcom lan 'das rnams ni/chos kyi sku rnyog pa med pas/shin tu rnam par
10a3(phal chen): thar pa thams cad la mthu chen po brnyes shing /pha rol tu phyin pa dam pa brnyes pas shin tu rnam par dgag pa yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi shin
10a4(phal chen): tu rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan dangs rnams kyi mdzad pa/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 'byung zhing dus thams cad du gnas pa ste/
10a5(phal chen): bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas rjes su dran pa'i thob pa'i sems can rnams la dus thams cad du/sangs rgyas bstan pa dang /sems can sems can shes dang ldan pa rnams la/dus thams cad du chos bstan pa dang /
10a6(phal chen): sems can sangs rgyas la dad pa'i sems dang ldan pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba dus thams cad du bskyed pa dang /chos kyi skyon med par 'das pa'i sems can rnams dus thams cad du ye shes khong du chud par bya ba dang /'gro ba thams
10a7(phal chen): cad du sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa dus thams cad du rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa rnams 'jig rten gyi khams thaMd du dus thams cad du chags pa mi mnga' bar gshegs pa dang /seMn thams cad la thugs
10b1(phal chen): rje chen po dus thams cad du mi gtong ba dang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa dus thams cad du thogs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi sangs da rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul
10b2(phal chen): pa sna tshogs dus thams cad du thogasapa med pa dang /sangs d+yas bcom ldan 'das rnams chos kyi dbyings gnas pa'i sangs rgyas dus thams cad du 'byung ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom
10b3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi mdzad pa/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du 'byung zhing /dus thams cadu gnas pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs
10b4(phal chen): pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i tshul rnams ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan dangs rnams ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i skus ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad ba'o//sangs
10b5(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasani/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las tshad med pa brnyes pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni sangs rgyas kyi spyan gyi yul mnga' bas ye
10b6(phal chen): shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni dge ba'i rtsa ba tshad med pa/bsam gyis mi khyab pa mnga' bas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zadapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni/chos kyi char
10b7(phal chen): mngon par 'bebs pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi bzad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi yon tan dang /legs pa mang po mnga' bas ye shes rgya matso mi zad pa'o//sangs d+yas bcom ldan
10b8(phal chen): 'das rnams ni sngon gyi mtha'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i smon lam dang khyad par mnga' bas ye shes rgya ma tsho mi zad pa'o//sangs sad+yas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni phyi ma'i mtha'i mur 'gro ba thams cad sangs rgyas kyi phrin
11a1(phal chen): @#//las rgyun mi 'chad par sgrub pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can gyi rigs thams cad ma lus pa'i sems dang spyod pa la ye shes rgya mtsho mi 
11a2(phal chen): zad pa'o//sangs rgyas bco ldan 'das rnams ni thams cad mkhyen pa'i bsod nams zil gyis mi non pas ye shes rgya mtsho mi bzad pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag 
11a3(phal chen): par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho mi zad pa'i//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu/'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/pha rol 
11a4(phal chen): tu phyin pa thams cad kyis mngon par byung ba 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste/chos thams cad la mi mkhyen pa gang yang mi mnga' ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom 
11a5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yi no//'di lta ste/thams cad kyang thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yino//'di lta ste/thams chad kyang stobs 
11a6(phal chen): bcu tshad med pa dang ldan pa 'di ni/sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yid no//'di lta ste/thams thams cad kyang bla na med pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo rab tu bskor ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi 
11a7(phal chen): tshul yin no//'di lta ste thams cad kyang sems can 'dul ba yang dag par zhugs pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam sa kyi tshul yin no//'di la ste/thams cad kyang 'gro ba thams cad kyi don du mngon 
11a8(phal chen): par sangs rgyas pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas mchom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul/'di lta ste/'dul ba'i sems can rnams 'dul ba la thogs pa mi mnga' ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis tshul 
11b1(phal chen): yin no//'di lta ste/mi gnyis par yang dag par spyod pa'i mtshan nyid kyi chos la rab tu gzhol ba 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yin no//'di lta ste ji ltar yongs su smin par gyur ba 
11b2(phal chen): dang /dul bar gyur pa'i sems can rnams la/sangs rgyas kyi yul mu med pas/sangs rgyas kyi yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba ston pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi tshul yi te/kye rgyal ba'i 
11b3(phal chen): sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tshul lo//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom
11b4(phal chen): tu bstan pa'i sgo rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams ni sems can gyi khams shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams 
11b5(phal chen): ni sems can gyi spyod pa sna tshogs shin tu bstan pa'i sgor tha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyasabacom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can thams cad la gdams ngag dang /man ngag shin tu bstan pa'i sgor ma tha' yas pa'o//sangs 
11b6(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams gzhog pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o //sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adasarnams ni byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thams cad la gdams ngagadang /man ngag gis yongs su 
12a1(phal chen): @#//bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad ma lus pa'i 'du ba dang /'jig pa shin tu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yas pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams
12a2(phal chen): kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa ni byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pa dang /mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongasu bstan pa'i sgor mtha' yasapa'o//sangs rgyas bcom 
12a3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi 'jig rten gyi khams
12a4(phal chen): ma lus pa thams cad du 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa ni bskal pa yangs pa de dang de dag tu bstan cing brjod pa dang /yongasu bshad pas/sangs rgyas
12a5(phal chen): kyi ye shes thams cad khong du chud par bya ba'i sgor mtha' yas pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi chos mtha' yas pa shin
12a6(phal chen): tu bstan pa'i sgo'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sems can rnams la sgrub pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas
12a7(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sku yongasu bstan pa ni/sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis gsung yang sems can rnams la sangs 
12a8(phal chen): sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'dasarnams len pa mdzad pa yang sems can rnams las sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bsgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom
12a9(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub bo//sa dang /chu dang /me dang /rlung gis kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sang rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub pa//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni byin gyi rlabs
12b1(phal chen): kyi yul dang mangs dmigs pa thams cad yul du mdzad pas kyang /sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las sgrub po//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mtshan kyang sems can rnams la sangs rgas kyi phrin 
12b2(phal chen): las sgrub po//sangs rgyas macom ldan 'das rnams ni/zhing la dmigs par mdzad pas kyang sems can rnams las sangs rgyas kyi phrin las grub po//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par
12b3(phal chen): dag pas kyang /sems can rnams la sangasargyas kyi phrin las sgrub pa//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams mi gsung par bzhugs pa yang sems can rnams la sa sangs rgyas kyi phrin la sgrub ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu
12b4(phal chen): po de dag ni//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'i 'das kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las/sems can rnams la sgrub po////bam po drug cu rtsa bzhi pa//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni//
12b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos te/bcu gang zhe na/smon lam chen po la rab tu brtan zhing smon lam zhig pa mi mnga' bas ji skad du gsung pa de ltar mdzad cing gnyis su mi gsung 
12b6(phal chen): ba 'di ni/sems can gyi gtso bo'i cho sa dang po'o//phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i skal pa thams cad du/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la yon tan thams cad yongasu rdzogs par mdzad par rab tu brtson zhing skyid lug pa mi mnga' 
12b7(phal chen): ba 'di ni/sems canagyi gtso bo'i chos gnyis pa'o//sems can 'dul bar 'gyur ba gcig cig gzigs kyang 'jig rten gyi khams brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med par gshegs shing 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 
12b8(phal chen): du gshegs pa la thags thogs med pa 'di ni/sems can gyi gco bo'i chos gsum pa'o//zhe sdang ba'i sems can rnams la thugs rje chen pos snyoms pa dang /dad pa'i sems can rnams la thug rje chen pos snyoms pa
13a1(phal chen): @#//'di ni/sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos bzhi pa'o//gang gi tshe bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yang dag par zhugs nas bla na med pa yang dag pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon 
13a2(phal chen): par sangs rgyas kyi bar du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa sangs rgyas su 'gyur ro snyam du/byang chub kyi sems ma nyams pa 'di ni/sems can gyi gatsho bo'i chos lnga pa'o//dge ba'i rtsa ba 
13a3(phal chen): thams cad yang dag par sgrub cing thams cad mkhyen pa nyidu yongasu sngo bas 'jug gi/'jig rten pa'i rgyud la ma yin pa 'di ni/sems can gya gtso bo'i chos drug pa'o//sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjesu sngo na/sangs rgyas 
13a4(phal chen): kyis spyad pa'i sems su lus kyi las dang /ngag gi las dang /yid kyi las kyis slob cing nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa sa thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du yongasu sngo ba med pas/bla na med pa yang dag 
13a5(phal chen): par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu ma ngon par sangs rgyas pa 'di ni/sems cad gyi gtso bo'i chos bdun pa'o//byang chub sems dpa'i sems rnam par dag cing /'od gsal la dangs pa/thams cad 
13a6(phal chen): du snang zhing kun tu 'od 'byung ba/sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la snang zhing thams cad du snyoms pas thams cad mkhyen pa nying yongasu rdzogs par byed pa 'di ni/sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos brgyad pa'o//'jig rten pa'i bde 
13a7(phal chen): thams cad yongasu spangs te/'jig rten pa'i dga' ba thams cad la ma chags pa dang /'jig rten thams cad kyis gos pa med pa'i smon lam thams cad du mnyam pa nyid kyis 'jig rten thams cad bde ba thams cad dang ldan 
13a8(phal chen): par byas te/sdug bsngal dang /spros pa thams cad rab tu 'jom pa 'di ni/sems canagyi gtso bo'i chos dgu pa'o//sdug bsngal gyi mi bde ba thams cad nyams su bab ste/'gro ba thams cad kyi don du dka' thub kyis
13b1(phal chen): shin gdungs kyang bla bar sems bskyed pas/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs yongs su bsrung ba dang ldan pa dang /stobs bcu'i sa'i bar du/sangs rgyas kyi yul la mos pas mthar thug pa 'di ni/sems can gyi gtso bo'i 
13b2(phal chen): chos bcu pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sems can gyi gtso bo'i chos so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam 
13b3(phal chen): par spyod pa chags pa med pa rnams ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams ni 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du gshegs pas chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan
13b4(phal chen): 'das rnams ni/'jig rten gyi khams ma lusapa thams cad na bzhugs pas chags pa mi mnga'abar rnam par spyod pa yin /sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni 'jig rten gyi kakhams thams cad
13b5(phal chen): na bzhugs pa dang /bzhengs pa dang /bzhud pas chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adis rnams ni/'jig rten
13b6(phal chen): chags pa mi mnga' bar ranam par spyod pa yin/sangasargyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'jig rtenagyi khams ma lus pa thamasacad kyi dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs kyang /chags pa mi mnga' bar rnams par spyod pa yin /sangs
13b7(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha' dang /da ltar byung ba'i mtha' la chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sang rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam+sa 
13b8(phal chen): ni chos kyi dbyings ma lusapa thams cad tsam gyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor na bzhugs kyang chags pa mi mnga' bar rnaM par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasani sems can ma lus pa thams cad kyis sems dang 
14a1(phal chen): @#//dang spyod pa la gzigs shing thugs kyi skad cig gcig la/cho 'phrul chen po gsum gyis/sems can rnam par 'dul yang chags pa mi mnga' bar rnam par spyod pa yin/sangs rgyas bcom 
14a2(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni sku gcig gis kyang tshad med bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa chen por rnam par spyod kyang chags pa mi mnga' bar ranam par spyod pa yin/sadas rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sems can 
14a3(phal chen): gyi khams 'byam klas pa ma lus pa thams cad du/de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos ma lus pa thams cad kyi mthar thugs pa bsam gyis mi khyab par ston cing gsung yang /chags pa mi mnga' bar rnom par spyod pa yin te/kye 
14a4(phal chen): rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sad+yas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi sangs rgyas kyi rnam par sbyod pa chags pa med pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sadas rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi 
14a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnas kyi sku/mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po sna tshogs kyis rnam par brgyan pa ni/sadas rgyas 
14a6(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sku'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla na mad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi gsung yan lag gi mchog dam pa drug cu dang ldan zhing yan lag lnga brgyas yongs su bzung ba dang /rgyan brgya 
14a7(phal chen): stong du mas rab tu brgyan pa dang /dbyangs kyi yan lag mtha' yas mu med pas rnaMr dag pa dang /sadas rgyas kyi chos kyi don dang /tshig dang /yi ge brjod du med pa'i yang brjodu med pas rab tu tshim par mdzad 
14a8(phal chen): pa dang /bag tsha ba mi mnga' bas seng ge'i nga ro chen po sgrogs pa dang /'jig pa med pa chen pos bsnyengs pa mi mnga' bar gyur pa dang /chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cadu sems can thaMd la grags cing bstsal te 
14b1(phal chen): khong du chud par 'gyur ba dang /sems can bsam pa tha dad pa dang /dbang po tha dad pa rnam pa sna tshogs 'dul bar nye bar gnas pa 'di ni /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi gsung gi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//
14b2(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi thugs kyi phrin las ni stobs bcus brgyan pa/sangs rgyas kyi tinge 'dzin chen po thams cad kyi me tog shin tu rgyas pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa bco brgyad kyi rgyan gyis shin tu
14b3(phal chen): brgyan cing bsgrubs pas chags pa med pa'i spyod yul dang ldan pa/chos kyi dbyings kyi gzhi la rab tu rten pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos gting med pa rab tu brnyes pa/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi rgyan du
14b4(phal chen): gyur pa/dus gsum gyi chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa mtha'as cad kyi sems can kun gyi sems dang spyod pa thams cad skad cig gcig dang ldan pa'i shes rab kyi rab tu mkhyen pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom
14b5(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams kyi thugs kyi rgyan mchogs dam pa bla na med pa'o //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni 'od zer bye ba brjodu med pa rab tu 'gyed pa mdzad de/'od zer de dag re re la yang 'od zer
14b6(phal chen): gyi 'khor gyi dra ba brjod du med pa dang ldan pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad ma lus par snang bar byed cing /'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud thams cad mun pa med par ston pa sangs rgyas skye ba dang mi 'bral bas/
14b7(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi phrin las phyir mi ldog cing don yod pa/sangs rgyas skye ba brjod du med pas gnas pa med pa rang bzhin rnam par dag cing kun tu snyoms par 'byung ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi 'od zer
14b8(phal chen): gyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams bzhad mo 'dzum par ston na/zhal gyi sgo nas 'od zer kha dog sna tshogs kha dog du ma dang ldan pa bye ba khra khrig brgya stong brjod du med pa phyogs
15a1(phal chen): @#//bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad snang bar byed pa/sems can gyi khams tshad med grangs med bsam gyis mi khyab pa rnam par ston pa/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i 
15a2(phal chen): byang chub tu rnam pa gzhan du mi 'gyur bar lung ston pa/'gro ba thams cad du shin tu gsal ba 'byung ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi bzhad mo 'dzum pa bstan pa'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//
15a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi chos kyi sku ni tshad med pa ste/bsgribs pa med pas rnam par dag pa chos thams cad chags pa med pa/nyid kyi mthar thug pa/mtha' dang dpung med pa'i chos kyi dbyings la gnas 
15a4(phal chen): pa/'jig rten thams cad dang ma 'dres pa/'jig rten thams ca da dang 'dzi ba med pa/'jig rten thams cad kyi de kho na nyid la yang dag par zhugs pa/'jig rten thams can las gyen du 'phags 
15a5(phal chen): pa 'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos kyis yang dag par spyod pa/tshig gi lam dang bral ba/brjod du med pa'i mthar phyin pa/phung po dang /khams dang /skye mched dang bral ba ste/'di ni sangs 
15a6(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnamasakyi chos kyi sku'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla named pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnam ni 'od tshad med pa mnga' ba ste/'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du 'od ma 'dres shing 
15a7(phal chen): rgyan du ma dang ldan pa kha dog sna tshogs brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i rgyan dang ldan pa/'od kyi snang ba kun tu thogs pa med cing thams cad du chags pa med pa 'od kyi dkyil 'khor bsgribs pa med pa tshad med pa'i 
15a8(phal chen): mdzod du gyur pa 'od thams cad kyi 'byung gnas su gyur pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi 'od kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcos ldan 'das rnams ni kha dog mtha' yas 
15b1(phal chen): pa mnga' ba ste/kha dog yid du 'ong ba mnga' ba'o//kha dog 'od gsal ba mnga' ba'o//kha dog tshad med pa mnga' ba'o//sems can gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad kyi bsam pa dang mos pa bzhin du rnam par dmigs
15b2(phal chen): pa'i kha dog mnga' ba'o//de ma yin pa'i kha dog gzhan khams gsum du gtogs pa thams cad zil gyis gnon pa'i kha dog mnga' ba'o/kha dog thams cad kyi pha rol tu phyin pa brnyes pas/sangs rgyas bcom
15b3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni kha dog bla na med pa mnga' ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi kha dog rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni dge bas shin tu rnam par dag pa ste/
15b4(phal chen): 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs dge ba rin po che'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa la shin tu byung pa'o//chos nyid kyi rnam pa thams cad du khyad par yod pa dang /
15b5(phal chen): khyad par med pa'i spyod pas rnam par dag pa'i rgyan gyi ye shes skye ba thams cad nang du gtogs pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs bsgribs pa med pa'i rgyud rnam par dag pas 'jig rten gyi mi
15b6(phal chen): snyan pa thams cad kyis mi snyan pa brjod du med pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rigs dang rgyud rnam par dag pa'i rgyan mchog dam pa bla na myed pa'o//sangs rgyas bcam ldan

15b8(phal chen): ba/thugs rje chen po mnga' ba 'gro ba thams cad skyob pa yon gyi gnas kyi dam pa bla na med pa yongasu 'dzin pa'i nang na thugs brtse ba can phan par mdzad pa/sems can thams cad kyi don du ye shes chen po byin
16a1(phal chen): @#//gyis brlabs te/bsod nams chen pos yongasu 'phags pa 'gro ba thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi zhing du gyur pa/sems can rnams kyi yon tan dang /ye shes kyi phung po thams cad rnam 
16a2(phal chen): par 'phel bar mdzad pa ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi byams pa chen po dang /thugs rje chen po'i mthar thugs pa'i bsod nams kyi rgyan mchog dam pa bla na med pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/
16a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi rgyan mchog dam pa blan med pa'o//kye rgyalaba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' sgyur ba'i chos 
16a4(phal chen): rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasrnams ni chos thams cad la mnga' ba dang /chos thams cad la snang bar mdzad pa'i mnga' sgyur bas/tshig dang yi ge mtha' yas pa la 
16a5(phal chen): thogs pa mi mnga' ba'i spobs pa mnga' ba dang /chos tham sa cad ma lus par yongs su rjod pas mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyasbcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyaskyi mnga' chen pos

16b1(phal chen): tu bsre ba 'jig rten gyi khams gzhan dang /gzhan dag gzhan dang gzhan gyi phyogasu rab tu bzhag kyang /sems can gyi khams rnams 'khor cing rmongs par mi 'gyur la the tsom du mi 'gyur bar mdzad pas/mnga' che ba ste/'di

16b4(phal chen): thams cad rgyan thaMd kyis rnam par brgyan pa dang /mi mthun pa med cing nye bar nyon mongs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gcig tu yong su 'du bar sangs rgyas kyi zhing rnam par dag pa thams cad byin gyis
16b5(phal chen): brlab pa la mnga' che ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pas mnga' sgyur ba'i chos bzhi pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi 'dul ba'i

17a2(phal chen): ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i bzhud pas 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du bzhud pa la thags thogs mi mnga' ba dang /phyogs bcu'i chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud rgya mtsho 'byam klas

17a8(phal chen): kyi sangs rgyas kyi mnga' chen pos mnga' sgyur ba'i chos bdun pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasakyi spyan gyi skye mched kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gang sgrub pa de snyan gyi skye mched kyis yongasu grub par mdzado//

17b3(phal chen): grub par mdzad do//thugs kyi skye mched kyis sangs rgyas gyi phrin las gang sgrub ba de 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad kyi dmigs pa 'jig rten pa dang /'jig rten las 'das pa sna tshogs rnam par phye ba thams cad

17b6(phal chen): mdzad pa dang /sems can re re zhing yang lus sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa tsam du yangs par mdzad kyang /sems can de dag la gnod pa dang /mi bde bar mi 'gyur ba dang /de dag du yang sems can re re zhing 'jig rten
17b7(phal chen): gyi khams gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pas bskal pa grangs med pa zad par gnas par bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi khams grangs med par gom pa gcig gis phyin pas 'jig rten gyi khams de dag du sangs rgyas byung ba rnams
17b8(phal chen): kyi spyan sngar 'gro bar bya ba dang /sangs rgyas de dag byung ba'i tshe yang brjod du med pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud dang gling de dag na sems can gyi gtso bos brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo
18a1(phal chen): @#//bskor bar bya ba dang /ba spu'i bu ga de nas sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa 'byung bar bya ba dang /sems can gcig nas gcig tu brgyud
