19a8(phal chen): 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyasakyi thabs mkhas pa dang po'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni chos thams cad la gzigs pa med cing phan tshun du mkhyen pa med do//ma bcings ma grol ba dang /gzungs ba med cing bsags
19b1(phal chen): pa med de yongasu rdzogs pa med par yang thugasu chung la sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la mnga' dam pa yang brnyes te/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams chos thams cad la mnga'-i sgyur ba de yang y+daMg pa de kho na nyid ma nor ba rnam pa
19b2(phal chen): gzhan du mi 'gyur ba dang /chos thams cad bdag gi med cing yongs su 'dzin pa med la de kho na nyid kyi mtha' las rnam par mi 'da' yang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni mnga' chen pos chos kyi dbyings thams cad snang ba
19b3(phal chen): la'ang rab tu mkhyen pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'angs rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa gnyis pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni mtshan ma'i mtha' thams cad mi mnga' ste/mtshan ma thams cad dang mi
19b4(phal chen): 'grogs shing /mtshan ma'i mngon par shes pa thams cad dang /ma 'dres la de'i rang bzhin du ngo bo nyid med pa dang /ji ltar ngo bo nyid med pa'i sgra'i tshul thams cad thugasu chud kyang gzugs kyi mtshan ma'i sku gzugs
19b5(phal chen): rnam par dag pa grangs med pa dang /zhing gi mtshan ma dang /rnam par spyod pa'i mtshan ma zad mi shes pa rnam pa sna tshogs yang dag par bstan cing bshad pa'i ye shes kyi phung po mun pa med pa 'gro ba thams cad
19b6(phal chen): kyi sgron ma chen por gyur pas mun pa med pa'i snang ba ston pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas macom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyis thabs mkhas pa gsum pa'o//chags pa med pa'i mtha' ni sngon gyi mtha' yang ma yin/phyi ma'i
19b7(phal chen): mtha' yang ma yin/da ltar byung ba yang ma yin te/chos kyi dbyings su gnas pa ni 'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba yang ma min bsaM/de bzhin nyid kyi mtshan nyid kyis 'byung ba med kyang sangs rgyas 'byung ba rnams kyi
19b8(phal chen): rigs tshad med pa'i phyir shin tu bstan pa brjod du med pas 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung pa'i sangs rgyas bskye ba yang rab tu brjod cing kun tu gzigs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yul gyis de bzhin du gzigs pa 'di ni/
20a1(phal chen): @#//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa bzhi pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyi sku'i phrin las dang /gsung gi phrin las dang /thugs kyi phrin las 
20a2(phal chen): kyi mngon par 'du mdzad pa yang mi mnga' la yongasu blang ba mi mnga' bas bzhugs shing rnam par phye ba med pa'i mthar thugs pa ste/dor ba med pa'i chos kyi gnas kyi pha rol tu phyin la bgrang ba'i tshul las rnam par 'das kyang tshul 
20a3(phal chen): grangs med pa'i mdzodu gyur cing /bgrang ba dang mngon par sgrub pa mtha' yas pa dang /'jig rten pa dang 'jig rten las 'das pa rnam pa sna tshogs tha dad pa'i grangs kyi tshul thugasu chud pa ye shes bsgribs pa med pas gzigs pa 
20a4(phal chen): rnam te/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi sems can gdul ba rnam par dmigs pa la rdzu 'phrul sna tshogs tshad med mu med par mngon par sgrub pa mdzad pa 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs 
20a5(phal chen): rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa lnga pa'o//chos thams cad lta ba med pas/tha mi dad pa dang /bye brag med pa dang /'byung ba med pa dang /mtshan nyid med pa dang /sna tshogs med pa dang /blang ba med pa dang /dor 
20a6(phal chen): ba med pa dang /rang bzhin med par rab tu mkhyen kyang chos kyi rgyud shin tu nges pa yang dag par sgrub cing 'jig rten gyi tshul dang 'gal ba med pa dang /rang bzhi med pa yang yang dag par gzigs pas thams cad mkhyen pa 
20a7(phal chen): thams cad gzigs pa mkhyen par bya ba thams cad ma lus par gzigs pas chos la mnga' sgyur ba thams cad kyang rab tu ston la/de bzhin nyid kyi rang bzhin las kyang mi nyams pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/
20a8(phal chen): rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa drug pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni thugasu chud pa'i rnam pa gcig gis gzhan dang gzhan mkhyen par bya ba/chos de'i skyon med pa las rnam par ma 'das pa gnas 
20b1(phal chen): pa med pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par dag pa mnyam pa nyid kyi chos skyon med pas dus thams cad du gnas pa rab tu mkhyen te/de la yang nyi ma gcig gam/zla ba phye dam/lo gcig gam/lo brgya 'am bskal pa 'jig 
20b2(phal chen): pa dang /'du bas rjesu bsnyag pa dang /phyir phyogs pa mi mnga' bar dus tshad med pa dang /nyi ma gcig dang /yud tsam gcig gi dus dang /snga dro nas nyi ma phyed kyi dus dang /phyi dro nas snga dro'i dus dang /nyi ma gung 
20b3(phal chen): nas phyi dro'i dus dang /nyi ma 'grang pa'i dus dang /nyi ma bdun dang /zla ba phye kyi dus dang /zla ba dang /lo dang /lo brgya'i dus dang /bskal pa gcig gi dus dang /tshad med grangs med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dus nas/phyi ma'i 
20b4(phal chen): mtha'i mur thugs pa'i dus su chos bstan pa dang /chos kyi 'khor lo thogs pa med cing phyir mi ldog /rgyun chad par mi 'gyur /bral ba ra mi 'gyur /bar chad par mi 'gyur med par mi 'gyur /'jig par 
20b5(phal chen): mi 'gyur /g.yo bar mi 'gyur /ring du mi 'gyur /'khor de na gnas pa nyidu yang rgyun chad par mi 'gyur te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa bdun 
20b6(phal chen): pa'o||sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad rtag tu dus la bab par bzhugs pa ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das/rnams ni mi 'jig pa tshad med pa mnga' pa'o//tha dad 
20b7(phal chen): pa yang dag par mkhyen pa grangs med pa mnga' ba'o//tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa'i tshad med pa mnga' ba'o//tha ba dad pa yang dag par ma khyen pa zad mi shes pa mnga' ba'o//tha dad yang dag par mkhyen pa thogs pa med pa mnga' 
20b8(phal chen): ba'o||tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa mtha' yas pa mnga' ba'o//thun mong ma yin pa'i tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa mnga' ba'o//tha dad la yang dag par mkhyen pa mthar thug pa med pa mnga' ba'o//tha dod pa yang 
21a1(phal chen): @#//dag par mkhyen pa de kho na dang ldan pa mnga' ba'o//tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa tshig gi tshul thams cad rab tu rtogs pa mnga' ba'o//chos thams cad tha dad pa yang dag par mkhyen pa mnga' 
21a2(phal chen): ba'o//bsam pa dang mos pa dang /dbang po ji lta ba bzhin du/chos kyi tshul dang /rnam grangs tshul ji ltar rnam par dkod pa bzhin dus mtshams med par ston te/mdo sde bye ba khrag khrig brjod du med pa'i yang rjod 
21a3(phal chen): pa med pa rnams mdo sde de dang de la thog ma dang /tha ma dang /dpung gi tshul gyis yang dag par ston te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa brgyad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
21a4(phal chen): rnams ni ming med kyang /ming dang rigs su byung ba'i chos thams cad tshig dang /yi ges shin tu dbyangs pa'i dbyings mngon par sangs rgyas pa ste/ming 'das pa ma yin pa dang /ming ma 'ongs pa ma yin pa dang /
21a5(phal chen): ming da ltar byung ba ma yin pa dang /ming sems can ma yin pa dang /ming zhing ma yin pa dang /ming chos ma yin pa dang /ming yon tan ma yin pa dang /ming byang chub sems dpa' ma yin pa dang /ming 
21a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas ma yin pa dang /ming grangs ma yin pa dang /ming drangs med pa ma yin pa dang /mid skye ba ma yin pa dang /mi 'gag pa ma yin pa dang /mig med pa ma yin pa dang ming yod pa ma yin pa dang /mang gcig pa ma
21a7(phal chen): yin pa dang /ming tha dad pa ma yin pa dang /min thams cad ma yin pa dang /chos thams cad rang bzhin gyis brjod du med pa dang /phyogs med pa dang /brda med pa dang /tshig gi lam med pa dang /sgra dang bral ba/sgra'i rnam pa 
21a8(phal chen): thams cad mi 'byung ba yongs su bsgrubs pa med pa/byas pa dang rnam par byas pa med pa/rnam pa gcig ma yin pa/rnam pa mang po ma yin pa yang thugs su chud la brjod pa dang /spyad pa thams cad la yang pha rol tu phyin pa'i mchog
21b1(phal chen): brnyes pa dag ste/rnam par rtog pa'i yul mi mnga' ba dag go/mngon par 'du mdzad pa med pa la rab tu brtson pa dag go/rtog pa dang kun tu rtog pa dang yongs su rtog pa 'gag pa dag go/spyod pa thams cad la mi gnas 
21b2(phal chen): par spyod pa dag ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa dgu pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni chos thams cad kyi gzugs ma skyes pa rab tu mkhyen pa ste/tshor ba'i 
21b3(phal chen): spros pa med pa dang /'du shes kyi grangs med pa dang /'du byed kun tu rgyu ba med pa dang /rnam par shes pa nye bar len pa med pa dang /rang bzhin gyis zhi bar rab tu mkhyen te/chos thams cad skye mched kyi rgyud med pa dang /khams
21b4(phal chen): rnams mi dmigs pa dang /chos thams cad mi 'jigs pa dang /chos thams chad bskyed du med cing rang bzhin gyis namakha' lta bu dang /chos thams cad dben pa dang /las med pa dang /rnam par smin 
21b5(phal chen): pa med pa dang /spyod pas 'byung ba med pa dang /ma grub pa dang /ma 'byung ba dang /grangs kyi mi brjod pa dang /yod pa ma yin pa dang /med pa ma yin pa dang /ma skyes pa dang /ma 'gags pa dang /kun nas 
21b6(phal chen): nyon mongs pa med pa dang /rnam par byang ba med pa dang /skyon med pa dang /gnas med pa dang /yongs su gnas pa med pa dang /yongs su smin par byas pa med pa dang /rnam bar btul ba med pa dang /sems can med pa dang /
21b7(phal chen): sems can med pa ma yin pa dang /srog med pa dang /srog med pa ma yin pa dang rkyen med pa dang /rkyen med pa ma yin pa dang/rten pa med pa dang /rten pa med pa ma yin par chos thams cad thugs su chud kyang chos kyi dbyings 
21b8(phal chen): thams cad shin tu bstan pa la pha rol tu phyin pa'i dam pa brnyes pa dag ste/nges pa dang ma nges pa dang /log par nges pa'i phung po gsum rnam par dgod pa rnams la stobs bcus thugs su chud pa'i ye shes mi 'jigs pa 
22a1(phal chen): @#//bzhis rnam par dag pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yul chen po dang ldan pas seng ge'i sgra sgrog pa 'di ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi thabs mkhas pa bcu pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras
22a2(phal chen): bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgya kyi thabs mkhas pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams kyi sadas rgyas kyi phrin las rgya chen po 
22a3(phal chen): tshad med pa bsam gyis mi khyab pa rnams te/de dag ni sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs ma gtogs par lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten dang /'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas 
22a4(phal chen): thams cad kyis 'jug par mi nus pa'o//bcu gang zhe na/phyogs bcu'i nam mkha'i dbyings kyi mthas gtugs pa'i chos kyi dbyings thams cad kyi 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du dga' ldan
22a5(phal chen): du skye ba bstan pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mu med pa dag la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/kha dog tshad med pa gzi brjid tshad med pa/snang ba tshad med pa/dbyangs tshad med pa
22a6(phal chen): dbyangs kyi yan lag rnam par dag pa tshad med pa/gsung brjod pas rnam par dmigs pa tshad med pa'i tinge 'dzin tshad med pa la spyod pa/shes rab dang /ye shes yul tshad med pa mnga' ba'i rnams lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig
22a7(phal chen): rten la phan 'dogs pa mdzad de/bdud dang bcas pa/tshangs pa dang bcas pa/dge sbyong dang bram ze'i skye dgu dang bcas pa/lha dang mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten la phan 'dogs pa mdzod do//byams pa 
22a8(phal chen): chen po'i spyod yul thogs pa med pa dang /thabs thugs rje chen po'i sgo'i mthar thugs pas sems can la phan 'dogs pa mdzado do//bsod nams kyi stobs dang /mnga' (bang)phun sum tshogs pa dang /dbang po rnam par 
22b1(phal chen): sbyang pa dang /cang shes kyi sems yongasu dag pas kyang sems can rnams la phan 'dogs pa mdzad do//theg pa sna tshogs rnams par dag pas kyang sems can dang rnams la phan 'dogs par mdzad do//theg pa chen po 
22b2(phal chen): yongasu rdzogs par bya bas kyang sems can rnams pa phan 'dogs par mdzad de//'gro ba thams cad gdon pas kyang sems can rnam la phan 'dogs pa mdzad/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi 
22b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi phrin las dang po'o//dga' ldan gyi pho brang nas 'phos pa med kyang yum gyi skur gshegs shing /byang chub sems dpa'i sku tha ma skye bar bstan pa/sgyu ma lta bur yongs su spyod pa sprul pa lta bu'i tinge 'dzin 
22b4(phal chen): gyi mthar thug pa/mig yor lta bur rnam par dmigs pa/nam mkha'i yul tshad med par khyab pa/bzhed pa bzhin du skye yang chags pa/mi mnga' ba/smig rgyu blta bur bstan pa/nyon mongs pa med pa'i 
22b5(phal chen): chos la yang dag par gzhol ba/gnas med pa'i ye shes la gnas pa rang bzhin gyis 'dod chags dang bral bas rnam pa dag pa/rgyan shin tu rgya che ba'i mdzod kyis sangs rgyas kyi phrin las
22b6(phal chen): mdzad do//khang pa brtsegs pa rin po che las byas pa'i pho brang chen po rin po che'i rgyan mang po ldan pa bzhugs pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//rdzu 'phrul gyis kyang sangs rgyas kyi phris las 
22b7(phal chen): mdzad do//yid la bya ba byin gyis brlabs chen pos kyang sangs rgyas kyi phri las mdzad do//rnam par 'phrul ba'i mdzod chen po yang dag par bstan pas kyang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzod do//ye shes kyi 
22b8(phal chen): nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor chen pos kyang sangs rgyas gyi phrin las mdzad do//yum sku skyed pa'i sku na bzhugs pas kyang sangs rgya kyi phrin las mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi yul gyi spyod yul chen po brnyes pas kyang sangs 
23a1(phal chen): @#//rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis 'jig rten byin du brlabs pa mtha' yas pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//tinge 'dzin chen po grangs med pa la rnam par 
23a2(phal chen): spyod cing snyoms par 'jug pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad do//tinge 'dzin las bzhengs pa de dang de dag gis kyang sangs rgyas kyi phris las mdzad do//spyod pa'i thog ma la yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa chen po'i 
23a3(phal chen): mthar thugs pa'i bar du thog ma dang /dbus dang tha ma'i tshul gyis kyang sangs rgyas kyi phri las mdzad do//sa 'di la gnyis su med pa thams cad mthar phyin par byas nas tha mar 'jig rten gyi grangs gyis skye ba yongs su 
23a4(phal chen): ston te /skye bas kyang 'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las/mdzad do//gzhon nur gyur pas kyang 'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad do//byang chub sems dpar 
23a5(phal chen): gyur pas kyang 'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//mngon par 'byung bas kyang 'jig rten thamscadu sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do/mngon par 'tshang rgya bas kyang 
23a6(phal chen): 'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad do//chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bas kyang 'jig rten thamasacad du sangs rgyas kyi phris las mdzad do//thabs mkhas pa sna tshogs kyis kyang 'jig rten sna tshogs 
23a7(phal chen): 'jig rten tshad med pa 'jig rten tu mtha' yas pa/phyogs kyi dra ba'i rgyud dang /rigs thams cad kyi 'jig rten ma lus pa thams cad du sangs rgyaskyi phrin las mdzad do//zhing gi rgyud ma lus pa'i 'jig rten 
23a8(phal chen): du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//las kyi rnam par smin pa ma lus pa'i 'jig rten du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//sems can gyi rgyud ma lus pa'i 'jig rten du sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad do/skye ba 
23b1(phal chen): dang shi 'pho ba'i rgyud ma lus pa'i 'jig rten du sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//sprul pa chen po'i rgyud dang /sangs rgyas kyi rigs dang /chos dran pa'i sgo'i 'khor los kyang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzod do//
23b2(phal chen): 'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gnyis pa'o//dge bar spyod pa'i las rnam par dag cing /gdung rabs thams cad du ye shes kyi tshul rnam par dag pas sku tshe'i rgyud kyi tshul dang ldan
23b3(phal chen): pa/sems can thams cad gdul ba thugs su chud pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad yongs su rdzogs par mdzad cing btsun mo'i 'khor gyi nang na bzhugs pa ston kyang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi 'dod chags dang bral ba'i tshul 
23b4(phal chen): gyi gnas la bzhugs pa rnams sgra dang /tshig thams cad dang bral bar brtags pas dngos po thams cad dngos po med pa las byung bar khong du chud par bya ba dang /dngos po thams cad shes pa la gzung 
23b5(phal chen): par bya ba dang /tshul khrims thams cad thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi mthar thugs par yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /snying rje chen pos 'gro ba mu med pa la rnam par blta bar bya ba dang /byams 
23b6(phal chen): pa chen pos sems can med pa'i yul la rab tu rtog par bya ba dang /dga' ba chen pos 'jig rten brjod du med par khong du chud par bya ba dang /btang snyoms chen pos sems la dbang sgyur ba la gnas par bya ba dang /
23b7(phal chen): thams cad mkhyen pa'i yon tan gyis rang yongs su rgyas par byed par bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi lus ma lus pa mngon par sgrub par bya ba dang /thams cad du gnas med pa'i lus yongs su rdzogs par bya ba dang /
23b8(phal chen): 'khor thams cad rnam par dag par nan tan du byed par bya ba dang /cho bstan pa rnam par brjod pa rab tu brjod par bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi dngos po la smod par bya ba dang /'gro ba thams cad kyi nyes pa la rtog par bya 
24a1(phal chen): @#//ba mdzad cing spyod pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa bas rnam par smin pa yang dag par bstan pa dang /ji ltar bkod pa'i tshul du sems can gdul ba dang /yongs su ma smin pa'i sems can yongs su smin par 
24a2(phal chen): bya ba dang /yongs su smin pa rnams tshul bzhin du gdul pa dang /grangs med pa'i thabs mkhas pa mngon par sgrub pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las phyir mi ldog pa yongs su bstan pa dang /sgo sna tshogs nas chos kyi spyod pa rab tu 
24a3(phal chen): brjod pa dang /sems can rnams gyi bsam pa sna tshogs rnam par sbyang ba dang /thugs rje chen po las byung ba'i chos kyi sprin gyis mngon par spang ba dang /thams cad la skyob pa'i byams pa chen po bskyed pa'i cho 'phrul gsum 
24a4(phal chen): gyis yongs su brlan pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad mthong ba dang /rnam par dmigs pa'i btsun mo'i 'khor gyi nang du bstan pa yan cad du 'jig rten mtha' dang dbung med par sangs rgyas kyi phrin las 
24a5(phal chen): yongs su bstan pa mdzad cing /ye shes mthu chen po thogs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyis mngon par mkhyen pa bsgribs pa med par bsgrub sa pa dang /chos rnam pa gsum gyis thabs mkhas pa dang ldan pa'i 
24a6(phal chen): phrin las kyi mtha' dang /sku'i phrin las kyi mtha' shin tu rnam par dag pa dang /gsung gi phrin las ye shes nyams pa med pas rnam par dag pa dang /thugs kyi phrin las kyi mtha' zab cing chags pa med pa'i mthar thugs 
24a7(phal chen): pa'i thabs la mkhas pas sems can rnams kyi don mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las gsum pa'o////bam po drug cu lnga pa/rnam par rgyal ba chen po mnga' 
24a8(phal chen): ba rnams ni btsun mo'i 'khor rgyan rnam par phye ba tshad med pa dang ldan pa/yid du 'ong ba spangs te mngon par 'byung ba'i spyod pa yongs su bstan pas sems can rnams 'jig rten gyi gnas las bskyod pa dang /'jig 
24b1(phal chen): rten gyi mngon par zhen pa rnam par bzlog pa dang /'jig rten du gtogs pa thams cad mi rtag par shes par bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi tshul la mngon par zhen pa rnam pa bzlog pa dang /'jig  rten gyi dga' ba la sred
24b2(phal chen): pa yongs su bcad pa dang /'jig rten nyon mongs pa byang zhing nub pa la yang dag par spyod par bya ba dang /sems can gyi don rjesu dran par bya ba'i phyir/mngon par 'byung ba'i don la rab tu gzhol ba dang /khyim pa'i
24b3(phal chen): tshul yongasu spang pa bstan pa dang /nyon mongs pa med pa'i chos kyi sbyor pa la yang dag par gnas par bya ba dang /sngon gyi smon lam yongasu rdzogs par bya ba dang /yon tan gyi dkyil 'khor tshad med pa yong+sa
24b4(phal chen): su snang bar bya ba dang /ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor chen po brnyes pas sems can rnams kyi mi shes pa'i sgrib pa rab tu bsal ba dang /bdag nyid bsod nams kyi zhing bla na med par gyur par 'gro ba thams cad
24b5(phal chen): la bstan pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad sangs rgyas la bskyed pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba'i nang du 'dus par 'gro ba thams cad la yang dag par brjod pa dang /bdag nyid kyis ye shes kyi don gziDa 
24b6(phal chen): par bstan pa dang /sems can rnams rnam par dag pa'i sgor brjod pa dang /sems can rnam sa kha na ma tho ba med pa'i sgor sgrub pa dang /'jig rten thams cad du 'byung ba'i sgo rab tu bstan pa dang /thams cad mkhyen
24b7(phal chen): pa nyid kyi rgyal mtshan yang dag par bsgreng ba mdzad cing mngon bar 'byung ba ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bzhi pa'o//don gcig ma yin pa mkhyen pa dang ldan pa thams cad mkhyen
24b8(phal chen): pa'i ye shes kyi mthar thug pa rnams ni/chos thams cad mngon par 'tshang rgya bar bya'o zhes/byang chub kyi shing drung du byon te/byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya bas kyang bdud kyi dpung thams cad rab tu bzhom pa dang /sku'i
25a1(phal chen): @#//byin gyis brlabs kyi mdzod kyi chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad du rgyas par dgang ba'i phyir/byin gyis brlabs zad mi shes pa'i mthar thugs pas mtha' rnam par spangs pa spyod pa tshad 
25a2(phal chen): med pa rab tu brnyes pa thams cad mkhyen pa'i yul gyis rnam par rol pa'i ye shes kyi don dang ldan pa/thams cad mkhyen pa'i yon tan yang dag par sgrub pa yongasu rdzogs pa/'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad 
25a3(phal chen): du gdan rnam par bkram pas yongasu rgyas pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing ma lus pa thams cad du/byang chub sems dpa' rnam par dmigs pa/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa dam pa thob pa rnams kyis zhal du blta ba/byang chub 
25a4(phal chen): sems dpa' thams cad kyi rnam pa dang /smon lam khyad par can dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad kyis yang dag par bsam pa'i chos kyi 'khor lo zab mo 
25a5(phal chen): rab tu skor ba/sangs rgyas kyi yul tshad med pa bsams pas/byang chub ba sems dpa' brjod du med pa'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa/byang chub sems dba'i spyad pa sna tshogs spyod pa la yang dag par 
25a6(phal chen): gzhol ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i 'khaur gyi dkyil 'khor rnam par dag pa mnga' ba 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis byin du rlob pa'i yul mnga' ba/dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa'i sems can gyis
25a7(phal chen): rnam par dmigs pa dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad tha mi dad pa dang ldan pa 'byung ba'i sa byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa mtha' yas pa'i mthu'i gnas la yon tan gyi khyad par thams cad rab tu brnyes pas bzhugs pa 'jig rten
25a8(phal chen): gyi rnam pa thams cad ma lus pa'i mthar thugs pa/sems can gyi rnam pa thams cad dang /zhing gi rnam pa thams cad dang /chos kyi rnam pa thams cad dang/byang chub sems dpa'i rnam pa thams cad dang yongs su smin par 
25b1(phal chen): bya ba'i rnam pa thams cad dang /dus kyi rnam pa thams cad dang 'dul ba'i rnam pa thams cad dag /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul ba'i rnam pa thams cad ma lus par mthar thug pa'i rnams 'gro ba ma lus pa thams cad 
25b2(phal chen): du mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par 'tshang rgya bas/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las lnga pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi chos kyi 
25b3(phal chen): 'khor lo bskor ba mdzad pa phyir mi ldog pa'i tshul gyis phyir mi ldog pa'i 'khor lo dang /'jig rten thams cad kyis khong du chud par 'gyur ba'i tshul gyis tshad med pa'i 'khor lo dang /mi 'jigs pa'i seng ge'i nga ro chen 
25b4(phal chen): po'i tshul gyis thams cad ma lus par mngon par sangs rgyas pa'i 'khor lo dang /chos kyi mdzod mun pa med pa'i sgos snang bar byed pa'i tshul gyis chos thams cad mkhyen pa'i mdzod kyi 'khor lo dang /
25b5(phal chen): ye shes nam mkha'i dbyings ltar tshad med pa'i tshul gyis chags pa med pa'i 'khor lo dang chos thams cad kyi dngos po med pa'i rang bzhin rab tu rtogs par bya ba'i tshul gyis bsgribs pa med pa'i 
25b6(phal chen): 'khor lo dang /sems can thams cad kyi chos kyi mig rnam par sbyong ba'i tshul gyis 'jig rten thams cad kyi sgron ma'i 'khor lo dang /'jigs rten thams cad du rgyas par 'gengs pa'i tshul gyis thams cad mkhyen pa'i chos 
25b7(phal chen): brjod pa'i 'khor lo dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad dang mi 'gal ba'i tshul gyis/sangs rgyas thams cad 'dab 'dres par gyur pa'i 'khor lo dang /de dag la sogs pa rnam pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du chos kyi 
25b8(phal chen): 'khor lo bskor bas sems can thams cad kyi sems dang spyod pa'i rnam pa chos kyi 'khor lo bskor te/de ltar chos kyi 'khor lo bskor bas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bsam 
26a1(phal chen): @#//gyis mi khyab pa mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sangs rgyas kyi phrin las drug pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams ni grong dang grong khyer dang /grong rdal dang /rgyal po'i 
26a2(phal chen): gnasu gshegs pas kyang sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de mi'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /lha'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /klu'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /gnod sbyin gyi rgyal po'i gnas dang /dri za'i rgyal po'i 
26a3(phal chen): gnas dang /lha ma yin gyi rgyal po'i gnas dang/namakha' lding gi rgyal po'i gnas dang /mi'am ci'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /lto 'phye chen po'i rgyal po'i gnas dang /srin po'i rgyal po'i gnas dang/sha za'i rgyal po'i gnas 
26a4(phal chen): dag tu gshegs kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasi rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi phrin las 'dzad de/'di lta ste grong khyer gyi sgor byon na/sa g.yo ba dang /'od zer snang ba dang /long ba rnams mig 'thob pa dang /
26a5(phal chen): 'on pa rnams sgra thos par 'gyur ba dang /smyos pa rnams dran pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dang /gos med pa rnams gos 'thob par 'gyur ba dang/sdug bsngal ba rnams bde ba 'thob pa dang /rol mo'i cha spyad ma 
26a6(phal chen): dkrol ma brdungs par sgra yid du 'ong bar 'byin pa dang /rgyan snod na 'dug pa dang /gnas nas ma phog pa dang /ma bskyod ma dkrol bar bskyod cing dkrol ba'i sgra 'byung ba dang /bya sna tshogs mang po'i sgra skad yid du 'ong ba 'byung ba dang /
26a7(phal chen): lta na gtang mi bra zhing mi 'thun pa med pa'i sku mthong bas kyang /sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po bstan pas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom 
26a8(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams sems cad rnams la sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las mdzad de/yongasu gzigs pa dang /rnam par gzigs pa dang /brkyang ba dang bskum pas kyang sems can rnams la sangasargyas kyi phrin las mdzad de/gshegs 
26b1(phal chen): pa dang slar bzhud pa dang /bzhengs pa dang /'chag pa dang /bzhugs pa dang /gzigs pas kyang sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//chos bstan pa dang /mi gsung bar bzhugs pas kyang sems can
26b2(phal chen): rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phri las mdzad do//rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul dang /yongasu bstan pa'i cho 'phrul dang /rjesu bstan pa'i cho 'phrul gyis kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams 'jig rten gyi khams kyi yul
26b3(phal chen): sems can bsam pa sna tshogs rgya mtsho dang ldan pa/'jig rten gyi spyod pa thams cad rgyu zhing 'byung ba dang ldan pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba chen pos shin tu bstan pa/sangs rgyas rjesu dran pa dang ldan pa/sngon 'grogs 'grogs
26b4(phal chen): pa byang chub sems dpa' dang gnas pa'i sems can rnams la sang rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad//yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams ni sku gzugs blta na mi ngoms pa'o zhes bya bar
26b5(phal chen): gzhol ba dang /sangs rgyas rnams skye bar dkon no zhes bya bar yid ches par byed pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mthong zhing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung thos pa ni bsod nams kyi chos
26b6(phal chen): dkar po'i 'byung gnas su gyur pa'o zhes sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa yid la byed pa dang /'jig rten thams cad rnam par dag pa ni/de bzhin gshegs pa skye ba yin no zhes bya bar khong du chud par byed pa dang /sangs rgyas
26b7(phal chen): kyi yon tan bsngags pa ni mtha' yas pa'o zhes bya bar yang dag par lta ba dang /ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas 'byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs yang dag par sgrub pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba dang /mos pa'i tshul thams
26b8(phal chen): cad kyis/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams mnyes par byed pa dang /de gzhin gshegs pa rnams ni yon tan mtha' yas pa'o zhes khong du chud par byed pa de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems can thams cad la
27a1(phal chen): @#//mngon du gyur par rtog pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa ni sems can gyi rigs bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i gnas su gyur par sems pa dang /de bzhin gsheg pa ni zhing thams cad na snang bar 
27a2(phal chen): khong du chud pa dang /de lta bu'i dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad 'byung bas/'jig rten thams cad na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad do//dad par byas pa dang /
27a3(phal chen): phyag 'tshal ba dang /thal mo sbyar ba byas pa dang /rjes su bstod pa dang /spyan drangs pa dang /mchod pa dang /mngon du mthong ba dang /'dzum ba byas pa dang /sangs rgyas la dga' ba'i sems kyis mos par byas pa'i dge ba'i rtsa 
27a4(phal chen): ba'i sgo nas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams ni sems can rnams la sang rgyas kyi phrin las mdzadado//sangs rgyas rjes su dran par gyur pas kyang /sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams 
27a5(phal chen): ni sems can rnams la sangs/rgyas kyi phrin las mdzad de//sangs rgyas sa mthong bar 'dod par bya bas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom
27a6(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sku kha dog dang dbyibs kyi rnam pa tshad med pa bstan pas kyang /sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das rnams ni sems can rnam la sangs rgyas kyi phrin la mdzad do//de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung thos par bya 
27a7(phal chen): bas kyang sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni sem can rnams la sangs rgyas kyi phrin bsamadzad de/de dag la sogs pa/sangs rgyas bcom ldan das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rnam pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong 
27a8(phal chen): brjod du med pa dag 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad du sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /mthu dang 'dul ba ji lta ba'i tshul las mi 'da' bar 'byung ngo //de ltar sangs rgyas kyi phrin lasa'ajig rten 
27b1(phal chen): thams cad kyi sems can kun gyi sems dang spyod pa dang /bsam pa la gnas pa dge ba rgya che ba tshad med pa dag phyir mi ldog pa'i yul dang /snying rje chen pos rab tu bskul ba dang /sngon gyi smon lam gyis yongasu 
27b2(phal chen): skyo ba med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las mkhyen pa'i stobs dang shugs kyis 'jig rten thams cad du 'byung ste/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las bdun pa'o//sangs rgyas 
27b3(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams ni dgon pa la bzhugs sam/zhi bar bzhugs sam/dben par bzhugs sam/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa la bzhugs sam/tinge 'dzin chen po'i gnas la bzhugs sam/gcig pu 
27b4(phal chen): dgyes par bzhugs sam/sku mi snang bar bzhugs sam/ye shes zab mo'i gnas la bzhugs sam/sangs rgyas kyi yul gyi stobs tshad med pa la bzhugs sam/sku'i yongasu spyod pa mi mthong 
27b5(phal chen): bas bzhugs kyang rung ste/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phri las gzhan gyis mi tshugs pa'i ye shes dang /smon lam gyi tshul gyis mos pa bzhin du 'dul ba'i sems can rnams 
27b6(phal chen): la rgyun mi 'chad par 'byung ste/sangs rgyas lha yongasu tshol bas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad do//sangs rgyas klu yongasu tshol bas kyang sangasargyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi'achad do//sangs rgyas gnod sbyin 
27b7(phal chen): yongasu tshol ba dang /sangs rgyas mi 'am ci yongasu tshol ba dang /sangasargyas lto 'phye chen po yongs su tshol ba dang /sangs rgya srin po yongs su tshol ba dang/sangs rgyas mi dang mi ma yin pa yongs su tshol ba dang 
27b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas nyan thos dang /sangs rgyas rang sangs rgyas dang /sangs rgyas byang chub sems dpa' yongs su tshol bas kyang sangs rgya kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chang do//chos ston pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phri las rgyun 
28a1(phal chen): mi 'chad do//sangs rgyas gcig brjod pas kyang sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad do//sangs rgyas thams cad brjod pas kyang sangs rgya kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'tshad do//byang chub 
28a2(phal chen): sems dpa'i spyod pa'i khyad par dang/smon lam thams cad gcig tu brjod pas kyang sangs rgyasakyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chang do//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa gcig rnam par phye ste/spyod pa tha dad pa brjod bas kyang sangs
28a3(phal chen): rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi'achad do//'jig rten gyi yul sangs rgyas kyi yul du brjod pas kyang sangs rgyasa'i phrin las rgyun mi 'chadado//sangs rgyas kyi yul 'jig rten gyi yul du brjod pas kyang songs rgyas kyi phrin
28a4(phal chen): las rgyun mi 'chad do/yul ma yin pa sangs rgyas kyi yul du ba rjod pas kyang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin la rgyun mi 'chad do//nyin mtshan dang /yud du yan man dang /zla ba dang /lo 'grangs par/bzhugs
28a5(phal chen): pa dang /yongasu bzhugs pa nas de bzhin du bskal pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa'i bar du bzhugs pas kyang /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams sems can rnams la sangs rgyas
28a6(phal chen): kyi phrin las mdzad de/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasarnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi phrin las brgyad pa'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasar rnams ni dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams la 'byung gnas lta bu sems can thams cad
28a7(phal chen): kyi bsam pa kun la rnam par sbyong ba lta bu/sangs rgyas rnams la dad pa lta bu/sems khong du chud par bya ba la shin tu mos pa lta bu/dbang po rnams la sgrub pa lta bu/sems can skye dgu thams cad la 'dul ba lta bu/
28a8(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad la mos pa lta bu/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la 'byung ba lta bu/byang chub tu zhugs pa rnams la skul ba lta bu/gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog pa rnams la ye shes snang ba lta bu dag ste/sangs
28b1(phal chen): rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'jig rten mi rtag par ston pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun chad par brjod pa dang /phrin las mdzad pa'i mthar thug par bstan pa dang /chos kyi rgyud nyams pa med pa'i dngos 
28b2(phal chen): por brjod pa dang /srid pa'i tshul rtsa ba nas chad par brjod pa dang sems can thams cad skyo bar bya bar bstan pa dang /sangs rgyas gyi rjesu 'gro ba'i sems su 'thun par bya ba dang /sku tshe'i mtha' lta bur sems 
28b3(phal chen): can rnams kyis shes par bya ba dang /sems can rnams kyis 'jig rten thams cad du dga' bar bya ba med par bstan pa dang /sems can rnams la ma byon pa'i sangs rgyas gcig nas gcig tu brgyud pa mnyes par 
28b4(phal chen): gyis shig par bstan pa dang /sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das rnam sa chos kyi 'khor lo skor ba bstan tu mi rung par khong du chud par bya ba dang /'jig rten rnams kyi rtsol ba bskyed pa dang /sangs rgyas 
28b5(phal chen): la dmigs par brtson pa'i sems la dgod pa dang /yun du dran pa rnam par dag pas de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ba'i rnam par rtog pa bskyed pa dang /skyo ba'i dbang po phyir bzlog cing /
28b6(phal chen): yongasu dag par mdzad pa dang /sangs rgyas la mos pa bskyed pa dang /skyo bar gyur pa'i sems la byin gyis brlab par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas la dmigs pa'i rkyen 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad 
28b7(phal chen): du phyin par dgongs pa mdzad pa dang /sku thams cad bshig ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gdung gis sems can rnams yongasu gzung ba mdzad pa dang /'jig rten rnam par 'khrug pa dang /dregs shing 
28b8(phal chen): myos pa'i sems can rnams sdom pa la dgod pa mdzad cing yongs su mya ngan las 'da' bar ston pas kyang /sangs rgyas kyi phrin las rgyun mi 'chad te/'di ni sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi sangs 
29a1(phal chen): @#//rgyas kyi phrin las dgu pa'o//de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mya ngan las 'da' ba'i tshe/'jig rten thams cad na sems can rnams smre sngags 'don cing du ba dang /mya ngan byed pa dang /mchi 
29a2(phal chen): ma 'byin pa dang /yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas zhe 'am/thugs rje chen po zhe 'am/ded dpon chen po zhe 'am/phan par bzhed pa thugs brtse ba can zhe 'am/lha dang bcas pa'i 'jig rten gyi sems can thams cad 
29a3(phal chen): kyi skyabasu gyur pa/mgon du gyur pa/rten du gyur pa/bla na med pa bsod nams kyi zhing nub par gyur to zhes 'am/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni 'byung bar dka'o zhes rnam pa de lta bur sme sngags 'don pa dang /mya ngan 
29a4(phal chen): byed pa dang /sangs rgyas mthong ba rjesu dran pa'i sems can rnams kyi bsam pa dang /yid la bya ba de dang de dag gi sgor/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las tshad med pa rgyun mi 'chad do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
29a5(phal chen): rnams yongs su mya ngan las 'da' ba'i tshe/'di lta ste/sems can rnams kyi sems dad par bya ba'i dbang dang /sems can rnams dad pas mchod par bya ba'i dbang dang/sems can rnams lhag pa'i 
29a6(phal chen): bsam pa rnam par sbyong bar bya ba'i dbang dang //sems can rnams yongasu smin par bya ba'i dbang dang sems can rnams yongs su sbyang bar bya ba'i dbang dang//sems can rnams 'dul ba'i dbang dang /sems pa rnams bsod 
29a7(phal chen): nams kyi tshogs yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i dbang dang sems can rnams de bzhin gshegs pa la mchod pas dmigs par bya ba'i dbang dang/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten dam pa sna tshogs su brgyan pas/phyog+sa 
29a8(phal chen): dang yul sna tshogasu sems can rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba bsags par bya ba'i dbang gis sems can rnams kyi bsam pa dang /mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du sku gdung dang /ring bsel byin gyis rlob ste/lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin 
29b1(phal chen): dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /mi'i gnas thams cad du 'dul ba dang /mos pa'i tshul ji lta ba bzhin du sku gdung dang /ring bsel byin gyis rlob po//rang gi sku 
29b2(phal chen): rnam par phye ste/sku gdung dang ring bsel tshad med pa grangs med pa /dpag tu med pa/bsam gyis mi khyab pa brjod du med pa'i yang brjod du med pa byin gyis rlob po//de bzhin gshegs pa'i mchod rten gyis sangs rgyas
29b3(phal chen): kyi sku gdung 'jig rten thams cad du rnam par phye ba de'i yul dang /phyogs kyi klu'i gnas dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha mayin dang /nam mkha' ldi dang /mi 'am ci dang lto 'phye chen po'i gnas dang /mi'i gnas dang 
29b4(phal chen): srin po'i gnas dag tu/sku gdung dang ring bsrel mchod par byed par 'gyur bas/sku gdung gi ring bsel dang /dbu dang phyag sen gyi mchod rten dang /sangs rgyas bzhugs pa'i mchod rten la 'gro 
29b5(phal chen): ba thams cad nges par sangs rgyas dran par 'gyur te/chos la rab tu rten pa ra 'gyur ro//dge 'dun la dad pa rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro//yul dang phyogs kyi gnas de dang de dag tu'ang rgyun mi 'chad 
29b6(phal chen): par sangs rgyas mchod pa la brtson pa'i bsam pas sbyin pa yongasu gtong bar 'gyur ro//bsod nams byed par 'gyur ro//mtho ris de dang de yongs su dag pa rab tu 'thob par 'gyuro//rigs dang rgyud kyi bdag nyid che 
29b7(phal chen): ba rab tu 'thob par 'gyur//longs spyod chen po dang 'khor rnam par dag pa rab tu 'thob pa 'gyur ro//lha dang mi'i phun sum tshogs pa nyamasu myong bar 'gyur ro//log par ltung ba med pa'i lam yongs su rdzogs
29b8(phal chen): par 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas 'byung ba don yod pa rab tu sgrub par 'gyur ro//chos dkar po la gnas pa yongs su rdzogs par byed par 'gyur ro//khams gsum pa las 'byung ba'i lam yongs su sbyong bar 'gyur ro//
30a1(phal chen): @#//theg pa gang la dad pa'i smon lam gyis nges par 'byung bar 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa de dang de dag la drin du gzo ba rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams la

30a8(phal chen): rnams ni/sems can sangs rgyas kyi rjesu dran pa la brtson ba rnams kyi sems dang bsam pa brjod du med pa 'byam klas pa rnams la gnyisu med par spyod do//sang rgyas kyi chos thams cad kyi don ma lus par 
30b1(phal chen): mngon par sangs rgyas pas gnyisu med pa'i sku rnam par dmigs par mdzad do//'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad la rang bzhin gnyisu med par thugs su chud pa mnga' 
30b2(phal chen): ba'o||'byung ba dang /'byung ba'i mtha' gnyisu med pa dang /sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha' dang /da ltar byung ba'i mtha' gnyis su med par thugasu chud pa mnga' pa'o//sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad gnyisu med pas
30b3(phal chen): dus gsum gyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams chad gcig par rab tu mkhyen cing ston pa thugs su chud par mnga' ba'o//sangs rgyas gcig dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi gsung gnyisu med par thugasu chud par mnga' ba'o/

30b5(phal chen): med par thugs su chud par mnga' pa'o//'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad dge ba'i rtsa ba gcig ste/gnyis su med par thugasu

31b1(phal chen): dang /chen po dang /rnam pa sna tshogs rnam par gnas pa'i mtshan nyid kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams la bsam pa tshad med pa yongasu mi btang bas/sangs rgyas kyi ye shes 'bar bar bya ba dang /yongs su rdzogs par bya

33a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad kyi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi 'jig rten gyi khams tsam du 'khor kun tu rgya che zhing yangs pa'i 'khor yug chen po'i rdo rje'i ri dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rnam pa brjod

40b1(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni mthong ma thag tu sems can rnams myur du 'jig rten pa dang /'jig rten las 'das pa'i dbang bo rnam par dag par 'gyur ro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni mthong ba thag tu sems 
40b2(phal chen): can rnams myur du bsgribs pa thams cad byang ba 'thob par 'gyur ro//sangs rgya bcom ldan 'das rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa mthong ma thag tu sems chan rnams myur du snang ba 'thob cing mi 'jigs pa zhum pa med pa'i spobs pa rab 
40b3(phal chen): tu rtsom te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams kyi de bzhin gsheT pa mthong ma thag tu myur du 'byung ba'i chos rnams so//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu pa 'di dag ni/sangs rgyas
40b4(phal chen): bcom ldan 'das rnams kyis rjesu dran pa rnam par dag pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dis rnams kyi sngon gyi sbyor bas kyang /byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis rjesu dran par

40b6(phal chen): rol tu phyin pa yongs su rdzogs pas kyang /byang chub semasadapa' rnams kyi rjesu dran par bya ba yin/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das rnams ni smon lam chen po yongs su rdzogs pas kyang/byang chub sems dpa'
40b7(phal chen): rnams kyis rjesu dran par bya ba yin /sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni yang dag par bsgrubs pas kyang /byang chub sesam dpa' rnams kyis rjesu dranapar bya ba yin/sangs rgyas gcom ldan 'das rnams
40b8(phal chen): ni sngon gyi mthar sangs rgyas kyi tshangs par spyad pa la spyad pas kyang /byang chub sems/dpa' rnams kyis rjesu dran bar bya ba yin/sangs rgyas mchog ldan 'das rnams ni mngon par 'tshang rgya ba'i sgo nas kyang /
41a1(phal chen): @#//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis rjesu dran par bya ba yin/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni gzugs kyi sku tshad med mu med pas kyang /byang chub sems dpa' rnamasakyis rjesu dran

41a3(phal chen): ldan 'das rnams ni stobs bcu dang mi 'jigs pas kyang /byang chub ses dpa' rnams kyis rjesu dren par bya ba yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams kyis rjes
41a4(phal chen): su dran pa rnam par dag pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rnam par spyod pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar chos kyi dbyings 
41a5(phal chen): thams cad dang /dus gsum gyi sems can thams cad kyi sems sna tshogs dang /sems du ma dang /spyod pa dang /g.yo ba'i rgyud thams cad ma lus par thugs gcig gis thugasu chud 
41a6(phal chen): pa dang /dus gsum gyi ses can thams cad kyi las dang /las kun nas blangs pa sna tshogs cho ma cho dgu rnam pa tha dad pas mngon par 'gro zhing nye bar sogs pa mang nyung dang /rnam par smin pa tha dad pa ma lus 
41a7(phal chen): par thugs gcig gis thugs su chud pa dang /chos kyi dbyings thams cad kyi sems can gyi rigs sna tshogs dang /sems can gyi rigs du ma'i sems can ma lus pa thams cad 'dul ba'i dus ji bzhin du yongs su bstan 
41a8(phal chen): pa'i cho 'phrul dang /rjesu bstan pa'i cho 'phrul dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi cho 'phrul gyis byin gyis brlabs yang dag par bstan pa ma lus par thugs su chud pa dang /chos kyi dbyings thams cad dang /phyogs dra ba'i rgyud 
41b1(phal chen): thams cad dang /'jig rten gyi khams kyi rgyud thams cad kyi sems can thams cad kyi sems kyi mtshan ma bzung ste /sangs rgyas bstan pa dang /skye ba dang /rnam par dbye ba ma lus par thugasu chud pa dang /'jig 
41b2(phal chen): rten gyi khams thams cad kyi sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji lta ba dang /mos pa ji lta ba dang /'dul ba ji lta ba bzhin du rnam pa tha dad pa tshad med par skad cig tu/sangs rgyas bstan pa dang/rnam par dmigs par 
41b3(phal chen): bya ba dang /byin gyis brlabs pa ma lus par thugasu chud dang /'jig rten gyi khyams thams cad kyi sems cad pha rol kyi bsam pa la/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs yang dag par bstan pa ma lus par thugasu chud

41b5(phal chen): 'byung ba bstan pa ma lus par thugs su chud pa dang /chos kyi dbyings/thams cad kyi sems can thams cad kyi thams cad du 'gro ba dang /de dang der 'gro ba'i lam ma lus par thugs kyi skad cig gcig 
41b6(phal chen): gis thugasu chud pa dang chos kyi dbyings thams cad sems can rnams kyis/sangs rgyas rjesu dran pa phyogs thams cad du btang ba'i tshul ma lus par thugs kyi skadacig cig gis thugasu chud pa dang /
41b7(phal chen): chos kyi dbyings thams cad kye sems can rnams kyi mos pa dang /sems kyi tshad ji lta ba bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi sku kha dog mu med pa/dbyibs mtha' yas pa bstan pa ma lus par thug kyi skad cig gcig gis thuDa 
41b8(phal chen): su chud pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rnam par spyod pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras 
42a1(phal chen): bcu po 'di dag ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi sangs rgyas kyi tinge 'dzin tshad med bsam gyis mi khyab pa rnams te/bcu gang
42a2(phal chen): zhe na/'di ltar sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/chos thams cad la rtag tu mnyam par bzhag kyang /chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi sems can rnams la 'di skad cig gcig tu chos ston pa mdzad

42a4(phal chen): rnams kyang bdag med pa'i mtha' la gnas par yongasu ston to//sangs rgyas bcom ldan das rnams ni/chos kyi dbyings/ma lus pa thams cad kyi dus gsum la mnyam par bzhag kyang
42a5(phal chen): thugs skad cig gcig gi cha shas la'ang mnyam par bzhag cing rnam par spyod do//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas rnams ni chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa mthas cad kyi phyogs 'byam klas
42a6(phal chen): pa'i dra ba thams cad kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyud/rnam pa thams cad la mnyam bar bzhag kyang mi zad pa'i zhing gi mtha' la yang bzhugs shing rnam par spyod do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'da'as rnams ni/

42a8(phal chen): ston pa mdzad do//sangs rgyas bcom dan 'das rnams ni chos kyi dbyangs ma lus pa thams cad sku dang /gsung dang thugs kyis yongasu rgyas par rtag tu mnyam par bzhag kyang/sems can thams cad kyi sems
42b1(phal chen): 'byung ba'i mdzod sna tshogs la yang mos pa dang yid la byed pa ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par spyod do//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams ni/chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad kyi chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin la

63a3(phal chen): dag pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong /rin po che bdun gyi pad+mo rdul shin tu phra ba snyed la 'khod cing 'od chen po rab tu gtong ba mthong bar gyur te/de dag gi dpe byad bzang po re re nas

66a8(phal chen): mi 'dzin pa 'dzin par 'gyur robyang chub sems dpa'i dbang po la dga' ba med pa 'dzin par 'gyur //byang chub sems dpa' dang /spyod pa la thun mong du spyad pa mi 'thob pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//byang 
66b1(phal chen): chub sems dpa'i bsam pa yongs su ma dag pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa'i lhag pa'i bsam pa zhum zhing dma' ba 'dzin par 'gyur ro//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du sems bskyed pa yongs 
66b2(phal chen): mi 'dus par 'gyur ro//yongs su sgyid lug pas rab tu zhig pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro //las kun nas nyon mongs pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//rnam par smin pa ma dag pa 'dzin par 'gyu ro//ye shes kyi stobs 
66b3(phal chen): bdag nyid che ba ma yin pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro/ye shes kyi rgyud byang bar mi nus pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rgyun chad pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro/thams cad mkhyen pa'i gdams ngag 
66b4(phal chen): yongs su mi zin pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro sangs rgyas kyi byang chub tu 'grub par mi 'gyur ba 'dzin par 'gyur ro de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul dang ring ba 'dzin par 'gyur ro/bdud kyi yul 
66b5(phal chen): la gnas pa 'dzin par 'gyur rode bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la mi gnas pa 'dzin par 'gyur/byang chub sems dpa'i go cha la mos pa med pa 'dzin par 'gyur robyang chub 
66b6(phal chen): sems dpa'i gnas kyi 'jigs pa 'dzin par 'gyur robyangs chub sems dpa'i dge ba'i las kyis skyo ba 'dzin par 'gyur rolta ba dang the tsom la mngon par zhen pa 'dzin par 'gyuro//sems yongs su 
66b7(phal chen): rmongs par bsgribs pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro:/byang chub sems dpa' dang /'grogs pa'i las drungs phyung ba 'dzin par 'gyur rode bzhIn gshegs pa'i tshul khrims nyams par gyur pas tshul khrims ngan pa 'dzin 
66b8(phal chen): par 'gyur ro:/bzod pa'i sgo mthar ma phyin pas gnod sems dang gti mug dang /khro ba 'dzin par 'gyur robyang cub sems dpa'i brtson 'grus chen po las mi ldog par bya ba la zhum pa 'dzin par 
67a1(phal chen): @#//'gyur ro//tinge 'dzin mi 'thob par 'khrug pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa dang mi mthun pa'i phyogs shes rab 'chal pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//gnas 
67a2(phal chen): dang gnas ma yin pa la mi mkhas pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro/sems can gyi thabs la thabs mi mkhas pa 'dzin par 'byur ro//byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes la rnam par mi dpyod pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro/
67a3(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i chos kyis 'byung ba khong du mi chud pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro //byang chub sems dpa'i mig 'byam klas pa bcu'i 'grub pa'i dmus long lta bu 'dzin par 'gyur ro/sgrib pa med pa'i 
67a4(phal chen): chos kyi rgyud mngon par sgrub pa glen zhing lkugs pa 'dzin par 'gyur rodabang po gzhan dang nor pa'i dbang po 'dzin pas sna yongs su nyams pa 'dzin par 'gyur rosems can 
67a5(phal chen): pha rol gyi tshig gi rgyud mi shes pas lce'i sgrib pa 'dzin par 'gyur rosem can gang zag pha rol la zhe sdang ba sa las kyi sgrib pa 'dzin par 'gyur to//myos pa'i sems 'thob 
67a6(phal chen): par 'gyur ba sems kyi sgrib pa 'dzin par gyur rosdom pa gsum dang mi ldan pas las kyi sgrib pa 'dzin pa 'gyur ro//ngag gi nyes pa bzhis kun nas nyon mongs pas skye mched kyi sgrib pa 'dzin 
67a7(phal chen): par 'gyur ro//chags sems dang gnod sems dang /log par lta ba mang bas yid kyi las kyi sgrib pa 'dzin para'agyur ro//chos yongs su btsal ba la rku sems 'dzin par 'gyur robyang chub 
67a8(phal chen): sems dpa'i dmigs pa la thogs pa 'dzin par 'gyur no//byang chub sems dpa'i mthu la shin tu zhum pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa'i skyon med pa la sgyid lug pa 'dzin par 
67b1(phal chen): 'gyur ro//byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes kyi sgo snang ba rgyun 'chad pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro//dran pa'i stobs dang shugs nyams chung ba 'dzin par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa'i stan pa yongs
67b2(phal chen): su mi 'dzin par 'gyur ro//byang chub semasad pa'i skyon med pa'i gnas rnam par spong ba 'dzin par 'gyur ro //byang chub sems dpa'ichud mi za ba'i gnas la mi sten pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro //nyan thos
67b3(phal chen): dang rang sangs rgyas kyi skyon med pa yid kyis mchod pa 'dzin par 'gyur ro /'das pa dang ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i tshul dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs dang ring ba
67b4(phal chen): 'dzin par 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de ltar byang chub sems dpa' gcig la gcig ngan sems bskyed pa ni de dag la sogs pa sgrib pa'i chos kyi sgo brgya stong phrag bcu rnams
67b5(phal chen): 'dzin par 'gyur ro//deci'i phyir zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang chub sems dpa'i theg pa la zhugs pa rnams ni/phan tshun du ngan sems kyi sems skyed pa lta bur kha na ma tho ba che
67b6(phal chen): ba'i chos 'ga' tsam yang ma mthong ngo//de bas na rgyal ba'i sras dag byang cub sems dpa'i spyod pa myur du rdzogs par bya bar 'dod pa'i byang chub sems dpas chos bcu la brtson par bya'o//bcu gang
67b7(phal chen): zhe na sems can thams cad yongs su mi gtang ba dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad la sems su bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad mi spang ba dang /zhing thams cad la mi zad pa'i ye shes
67b8(phal chen): kyis 'jug pa dang /byang chub sems pa'i spyod pa thams cad la mos pa dang /dad pa chen po dang /nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas pa'i chos kyi dbyings mnyam pa nyid rnam par bsgoms pa'i byang chub kyi
68a1(phal chen): @#//sems mi gtang ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs la 'jug pas/sangs rgyas kyi byang chub la rnam par dpyad pa dang /tha dad pa yang dag par shes pa la brtson 
68a2(phal chen): par bya ba dang /sems can thams cad yongs su smin par bya bas yongs su mi skyo ba dang /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du gnas pa la mngon par chags pa med pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag byang 
68a3(phal chen): chub sems dpas chos bcu po de ga la mngon par brtson par bya'o//de chos bcu po de dag la gnas pas rnam par dag par byed pa'i chos bcu rab tu 'thob ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste chos zab mo 
68a4(phal chen): la 'jug pa rnam par dag pa dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang 'grogs pa rnam par dag pa dang/sangs rgyas kyi bstan pa yod/sa su bzung ba rnam par dag pa dang /nam mkha'i dbyings rnam par sgom pa 
68a5(phal chen): rnam par dag pa dang /chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa rnam par dag dang /mtha' dang dbus med pa'i sems rnam par dpyad pa rnam par dag pa dang byang chub sems dpa' thams cad 
68a6(phal chen): kyis grogs byang ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba rnam par dag pa dang/bskal pa thams cad la mngon par chags pa me pa rnam par dag pa dang dus gsum thams cad rab tu brtag pa rnam par dag pa 
68a7(phal chen): dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigs su nan tan bya ba rnam par dag pa ste/rnam par dag par byed pa'ichos bcu po de dag rab tu 'dzin to//de de lta bu'i chos bcu po de dag la gnas pas/ye shes 'byam 
68a8(phal chen): klas pa bcu la 'jug par 'gyur te/bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste sems can thams cad kyi sems dang spyod pa la 'jug pa dang /'gro ba thams cad kyi las kyi rnam par smin pa rab tu phye ba la 'jug pa dang /
68b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la ye shes snang bas 'jug pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi tshul dang ldem po dag la ye shes kyi 'jug pa dang /gzungs kyi sgo thams cad la ye shes kyi 'jug
68b2(phal chen): pa dang /ye shes thams cad la spobs pa'i ye shes kyi 'jug pa dang/sems can thams cad kyi skad dang /tha snyad dang /tshig la mkhas pa'i ye shes la 'jug pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams thams
68b3(phal chen): cad du lus bstan pa'i ye shes la 'jug pa 'ang /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad du gzugs brnyan 'byung ba bstan pa'i ye shes la 'jag pa dang /skye mched thams cad thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes
68b4(phal chen): la 'jug pa ste/ye shes 'byam klas pa bcu po dag rab tu 'dzin to//de de ltar ye shes 'byam klas pa bcu la rab tu gnas pas yang dag par gzud pa la 'jug pa bcu 'thob ste/bcu gang
68b5(phal chen): zhe na 'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad skra'i sbubs su gzud pa la 'jug cing /skra'i sbubs gcig 'jig rten gyi khams bsam gyis mi khyab par mngon par sgrub pa la 'jug pa dang /
68b6(phal chen): sems can thams cad kyi lus sems can gcig gi lus su bsdu zhing /sems can gcig gi lus brjod du med pa'i lus su mngon par bsgrubs pa la 'jug pa dang /bskal pa brjod du med pa sems kyi skad cig tu
68b7(phal chen): gzud pa la 'jug cing /sems kyi skad cig brjod du med pa'i bskal par 'jug pa bstan pa dang /chos gcig sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad du yang dag par gzhol ba la 'jug cing /sangs rgyas kyi chos thams
68b8(phal chen): cad chos gcig tu gzud pa la 'jug pa dang /skye mched gcig brjod du med pa'i skye mched du bya ste/brjod du med pa'i skye mchedadu bya ba la 'jug cing /skye mched brjod du pa skye mched gcig tu bstan pa la 'jug pa
69a1(phal chen): @#//pa dang /dbang po gcig brjod du med pa'i dbang por bya ba la 'jug cing /dbang po brjod du med pa dbang po gcig tu gzud pa la 'jug pa dang /dbang po thams cad dbang po med pa dang /
69a2(phal chen): tha mi dad par 'jug cing /dbang po med pa dbang por bya ba la 'jugs pa dang /'du shes thams cad 'du shes gcig tu byin gyis brlab pa la 'jug cing /'du shes gcig tu 'du shes thams cad du byin gyis brlab 
69a3(phal chen): pa la 'jug pa dang /tshig gcig rnam par brjod pa tshig brjod du med pa rnam par brjod par bya ba la 'jug cing /tshig rnam par brjod pa thams cad sgra gcig gi dbyangs su byin gyis brlab pa la 'jag pa dang /dus 
69a4(phal chen): gsum thams cad bsam gtan gcig tu bsdu ste /bsam gtan gcig po dus thams cad du kun tu spro bar bstan pa la 'jug pa ste /kye rgyal ba'i sras dag yang dag par gzud pa bcu po de dag sems 
69a5(phal chen): kyi bsam pa bcu la gnas te /rnam par dpyad par bya'o//bcu gang zhe na 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi spyod pa la mi spyod pa'i sems kyi bsam pa la gnas pa dang /'gro ba thams cad 
69a6(phal chen): kyi 'du shes dang /yid la byed pa la mi gnas pa'i sems kyi bsam pa gnas pa dang /lhag pa'i bsam pa nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas pa'i sems kyi bsam pa dang /chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus 
69a7(phal chen): med pa'i sems kyi bsam pa na gnas pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi dgongs pa la /mkhas pa'i sems kyi bsam pa ni gnas pa dang /chos zab mo tha mi dad pa la mkhas pa'i sems kyi bsams pa la gnas 
69a8(phal chen): pa dang /dogs pa thams cad la lan med par bya ba'i sems kyi bsam pa la nas pa dang /dus thams cad tha mi dad cing mnyam pa'i sems kyi bsam pa laganas pa dang /'das pa dang ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung 
69b1(phal chen): ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi sems kyi bsam pa la gnas pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi stobs tshad med pa'i sems kyi bsam pa la gnang pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag sems kyi bsam 
69b2(phal chen): pa bcu po de dag la gnas ste/byang chub sems dpas yang dag par gzung pa bcu po de dag rnam par dpyad par bya'o//de sems kyi bsam pa bcu po de dag la gnang pas/sangs rgyas kyi chos la mkhas pa'i snang 
69b3(phal chen): ba bcu 'thob bo//bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/sangs rgyas kyi chos zab mo snang ba la mkhas pa rab tu 'thob po//sangs rgyas kyi chos yangs pa mngon par sgrub pa la mkhas pa rab tu 'thob//sangs rgyas kyi chos
69b4(phal chen): sna tshogs shin tu bstan pa la mkhas pa rab tu 'theb po//sangs rgyas kyi chosamanyam ba nyid la 'jug pa la mkhas pa rab tu 'thib po//sangs rgyas kyi chosatha dad pa'i snang ba la 'jug par
69b5(phal chen): mkhas pa rab tu 'thob bo//sangs rgyas kyi chos tha mi dad pa la 'jug par mkhas pa rab tu 'thob pa//sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi rgyan la 'jug par mkhas pa rang tu 'thob po//sangs rgya 
69b6(phal chen): kyi tshul gcig pa'i mos pa la 'jug par mkhas dang rab tu 'thob po//sang rgyas kyi chos mtha' yas pa'i tshul la 'jug par mkhas par rab tu 'thob po//sangasargyas kyi chos thams cad rang gi sems la 
69b7(phal chen): rag lus pas rang gi stobs dang phyir mi ldog pa la mkhas pa rab du 'thob ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras dag sangs rgyas kyi chos la mkhas pa'i snang ba bcu po de dag rab tu 'thob po//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag de bas na/
69b8(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos chos de dag thos nas/rim gro dang zhe sa chen po'i bsam pas gzung bar bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/byang chub sems dpa' chos 'di la gnas pa la ni/bla na med pa 
70a1(phal chen): @#//yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyang dkon pa ma yin te/las gron chung ngus sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yang dag par 'grub pa'i byin gyis brlabs rab tu 'thob bo//'das 
70a2(phal chen): pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dang /mnyam pa nyid du yang 'jug par 'gyur ro//de nas de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi mthus/'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du 
70a3(phal chen): med pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong phrag bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed rnam pa drug tu rab tu g.yos so//sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang chos nyid rab tu thob pas lha rdzas las 'das pa'i me tog gi sprin gyi char mngon par bab po//
70a4(phal chen): lha rdzas las 'das pa'i spos kyi sprin gyi char mngon par pab po//gzhung de bzhin du phye ma dang /na bza dang /rgyal mtshan dang /ba dan dang /gdugs dang /nor bu rin po che dang /sbrul gyi snying po'i tsan
