70a6(phal chen): sprin gyi char mngon par dbab po/lha rdzas las 'das pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i ston gyi rnam pa brjod du med pa'i sprin gyi char mngonapar bab po//lha rdzas las las 'das shing chos kyi dbyings 
70a7(phal chen): su rgyas par khyab pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'o gsung gi dbyangs mngon par brjod pa'i sprin gyi char mngon bar pab po//'jig rten gyi khams yongs su dag pa rnam pa brjod du med pa yi sprin gyi char mngon par bab 
70a8(phal chen): po//byang chub nye bar brtan pa'i rnam pa brjod du med pa' sprin gyi char mngon par pab po//'od zer snang ba'i rnam pa brjod du med ba'i sprin gyi char gyi mngon par bab po//shin tu bstan pa'i byin gyi brlabs 
70b1(phal chen): kyi char rnam pa brjod du med pa'i sprin gyi char mngon par bab ste/ji ltar 'jig rten gyi khams 'di'i gling bzhi par /de bzhin gshegs pa byang chub kyi shing drung du de bzhin gshegs pa mngon par 'tshang rgya ba'i snying po 
70b2(phal chen): byang chub la byang chub sems dpa'i pho brang na bzhugs par snang ba de bzhin du/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa rgyas par gang bar bzhugs ste/chos bstan pa 'di 'ang thams cad du byung ngo//sangs 
70b3(phal chen): rgyas kyi mthu dang /phyogs bcur de bzhin gshegs pa mngonapar sangs rgyas pa'i chos nyid rab tu thob pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjod du med pa phrag bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyi sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'das pa'i 
70b4(phal chen): pha rol nas/byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra  ba snyed kyang 'ongs par gyur te/phyogs bcu bkang nas de dag 'di skad zer ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar de bzhin gshegs 
70b5(phal chen): pa'i smon lam lung bstan pa'i chos nyid brjod pa legaso legaso//kye rgyal ba'i sras bdag cag kyang thams cad kun tu bzang po zhes bya bar ming 'thun pa sha stag ste/'jig rten gyi khams 
70b6(phal chen): kun tu dpal zhes bya ba dag nas de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan kun tu dpal gyi dbang phyug ces bya ba sha stag gi drung nas 'dir phyin te/thams cad na yang chos bstan pa 'di nyid sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis 'khor 
70b7(phal chen): gyi rnam pa 'di lta bu dag tu tshig 'di dag dang /yi ge 'di dag dang /go bar bya ba 'di dag dang /brjod pa 'di dag gis lhag chad med par byung bar gyur te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bdag cig kyang dbang du gyur pa sha stag/
70b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs kyis 'khor 'dir phyin to/ji ltar bdag cag 'khor 'dir phyin pa de bzhin du 'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa kun gyi de bzhin gshegs pa mngon par 'tshang 
71a1(phal chen): @#//rgya ba'i bzhir/byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing bcu'i rdul shin tu phra ba snyed kyis phyogs bcu bkang ste phyin par gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' kun tu bzang pos 
71a2(phal chen): phyogs bcur blta ste/chos kyi dbyings la rnam par bltas nas/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa rab tu brjod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mngon par 'tshang rgya ba'i dbyings yongs su brjod pa dang /smon lam chen po'i 
71a3(phal chen): dbyings kun tu grags par bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi khams dang /bskal pa thams cad kyi grangs yongs su brjod pa dang/dus ji lta ba bzhin du sangs rgyas 'byung ba bstan pa dang /sems can gyi khams dbang po ji ltar 
71a4(phal chen): yongs su smin pa'i tshul bzhin du sangs rgyas byung ba mnyes par bya ba brjod pa dang /byin gyis brlabs pa'i tshul bzhin du sangs/rgyas 'byung ba dang /de bzhin gshegs ba'i spyod pa don yod pa yongs su 
71a5(phal chen): brjod pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba ji ltar bskyed pa bzhin du gsal bar bya ba yang dag par bstan pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' mang po'i mthu chen po bstan pa dang /sems cad thams cad la 
71a6(phal chen): rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi sgras bskul bar byed cing /dge ba'i rtsa ba'i stobs dang shugs dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag smras so//thams cad shin tu dad sems kyis//
71a7(phal chen): sgrib pa ma lus thams cad rnam par bsal gyis la//yon tan 'byung gnas 'jig rten gtso bo yi//smon lam dam pa bshad kyis mnyan par gyis//mkhas mchog skyes bu seng ge dag/spyad pa ci 'dra sngon spyad spyod par 
71a8(phal chen): 'gyur ba'i rnams//byang chub sems dpa' gtso rnams kyi//spyod pa thams cad rim gyis brjod par bya//'di la dpe med mchog rnams kyi//bskal pa'i grangs dang 'jig rten grangs dang chos bstan dang //rgyal ba rnams kyi 
71b1(phal chen): 'byung ba dag/yongasu brjod kyis dang pa'i yid kyis nyon//de dag sngan chad smon lam gyis//sems can ji ltar sdug bsngal rnams las dgrol bya zhes//sems can rnams la phan pa'i phyir//'jig rten sgron ma skye ba
71b2(phal chen): rnams kyang mthong//spyod pa ji ltar shes gyur te//gsung gi mchog rnams kun la mchod pa byas pas na//sangs rgyas mnyam pa nyid rnyed de//thams cad mkhyen nyid rab la mngon sangs rgyas//sngon gyi mi yi seng ge rnams//
71b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas phyogs bcur 'od snang rab tu 'bar mdzad cing//'od zer dra ba rab 'gyed pas//phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten snang mdzad mthong gyur nas//bdag cag rgyal ba'i yon tan gyis//brgyan pa'i 'jig rten sgron ma'i
71b4(phal chen): dam par 'gyur bya ste//stobs bcu mnga' ba kun mkhyen par//mi'i seng ger bya zhes smon lam 'debs//nged kyis sems can thams cad kyi//'dod chags zhe sdang gti mug rab tu 'bar gyur pa'i//sdug
71b5(phal chen): bsngal rnams las rab dkrol te//ngan song thams cad yongs su bcad bya zhes//'di 'dra'i smon lam rdzogs byas te//de nas dpa' ba chen po spyod pa rab la spyod//blo brtan phyir ldog mi 'gyur ba//stobs
71b6(phal chen): bcu rnams kyi chags med rab tu 'thob//skyes bu seng ge gang rnams kyis//'di 'dra'i smon lam sngar btab phyir ldog mi 'gyur bar//'jig rten rnams na spyod mdzad pa//de dag don yod spyod pa brjod pa bya//bskal pa bzang
71b7(phal chen): po gcig la yang//sangs rgyas stong 'grangs tshang bar kun kyang 'byung 'gyur te//kun tu gzigs pa gang brjod pa//de dag rim gyis bshad pa 'di 'dra'o//bskal pa bzang po gcig bzhin du//brjod du med pa'i bskal
71b8(phal chen): pa khrag khrig de bzhin te//sangs rgyas ma byon de dag gi/bzang po'i spyod pa bdag gis bstan par bya//zhing rgyud gcig po ji bzhin du//brjodu med pa'i zhing rnams thams cad kun bzang ste//ma byon stobs bcu'i
72a1(phal chen): /skyon med pa'i//spyod pa thams cad de bzhin bshad par bya//rgyal ba gang 'og gang 'byung dang //ci mtshan de'i smon lam rnam pa ci 'dra dang //rgyal ba gang gis gang gi lung //bstan 
72a2(phal chen): mdzad mkhas pa'i sku tshe ji tsam dang //chos kyi rnam pa dge ba ni//gang gis bsgrubs te dam pa brnyes par gyur pa dang //'jig rten ji ltar btul ba dang //rgyal ba rnams kyi dam chos cir gnas dang //rnam dag zhing 
72a3(phal chen): yang ci 'dra dang //sems can rnam par dag dang chos kyi 'khor lo dang //bstan pa'i dus dang dus min dang //de las gang zag rnam par dag 'gyur dang //sems can rnams kyi las gang dang //spyod pa gang dang rnam grol 
72a4(phal chen): ci 'drar 'gyur ba dang //dbang po rno rtul bar ma dang //spyod ma chog sems can 'dul ba'ang bstan par bya//'di 'dra'i shes par 'jug pa la//spyod pa'i dam pa rnams kyis rab tu zhugs gyur cing //
72a5(phal chen): bzang po'i 'byung gnas spyod pa dang //'gro kun ye shes 'khor lo spyod zhugs pa//lus kyi las la chags med cing //ngag la chags med sgra dbyangs thams cad rnam dag la//sems spyod chags pa med pa 
72a6(phal chen): yis//dus gsum ma lus rgyas par khyab par byed//'di 'dra kun tu bzang po yi//spyod pa'i mchog la pha rol phyin pa thob gyur pa//brjod du med pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams//chos dbyings kun tu rab tu brjod pa byed//ma 
72a7(phal chen): 'ongs dus kyi bskal pa dang //'byam klas zhing rnams brjod du med pa thams cad kyang //skad cig sems kyis rab tu shes//chos nyid de la rnam par rtog pa med//gang rnams spyod pa'i sa 'di la//spyod pa'i dam pas 
72a8(phal chen): rjes su zhugs par gyur pa'i rnams//byang chub sems dpa' mchog rnams kyi//yon tan spyod pa phyogs tsam bshad par bya//mtha' dang dbus med ye shes can//stobs bcu rnams kyi spyod yul mchog la zhugs 
72b1(phal chen): gyur pa//thams cad shes byed mkhas pa'i rnams//mi ldog spyod pas rab tu zhugs gyur cing //'od byed blo gros dam pa dang //kun tu bzang po'i smon lam mchog la zhugs gyur pa//dpe zla med pa de dag gi/
72b2(phal chen): ye shes tshul dang spyod pa brjod par bya//rdul phran gcig gi cha shas la//'jig rten khams rnams ji snyed de dag shes pa dang //spyod ba rgya che thos gyur na//'jig rten de dag thams cad mos par 'gyur//rdul phran
72b3(phal chen): gcig la ji lta ba//de bzhin rdul phran thams cad ma lus gzung bya ste//ye shes bzang po de dag ni//'jig rten khams la 'jug pa bsam mi khyab//de dag dus kyi rnam pa la'ang //rdul phran gcig gi khyon gyi cha la yang dag
72b4(phal chen): 'jug/rdul phran gcig la de bzhin du//zhing yangs rnam pa tha dad kun la 'jug/rdul phran thams cad re re la//zhing rnams brjod du med pa mang po 'dzud pa byed//rnam pa tha dad du ma dang //gcig
72b5(phal chen): dang tshad med de bzhin rab tu shes//ji snyed ci yang ma lus pa'i//chos dbyings gzung ba thams cad yang dag gzung byed cing //mtshan nyid tha dad sna tshogs te//sems can grangs kyi bskal pa
72b6(phal chen): rgyud mi mnyam//'jig rten phra bar 'jug pa la//phra la shin tu gzhol bas phra bar 'jug bya ste//brjod du med pa rab bstan pa'i//bskal pa thams cad ma lus mdzud pa byed//de dag ring dang thung ba dang //dus kyi skad cig
72b7(phal chen): sems can skal ba mi mthun shes//shes spyod mi mnyam ma yin pa'i//shes pas de dag thams cad khong du chud//yangs dang yangs min mkhas pas na//'jig rten zab mo rnams la rab tu jug pa byed//lus la zhing rnams
72b8(phal chen): de shes te//zhing la'ang de bzhin gcig min lus mdzud do//phyogs rnams bcu po thams cad du//phyogs rnams kun la 'jug cing skad cig dus dang //'jig rten khams rnams 'byam klas la//tshad med gzi brjid ldan
73a1(phal chen): @#//pa rab tu 'jug/gang yang zhing rnams mtha' yas shing //dbus med gzung ba byas shing dus kyi dbyings su 'dzud//zhing gi rgyud la mkhas pa'i rnams//shes dang ldan pas ye shes rab tu 'byung //
73a2(phal chen): bzang po'i yon tan ldan pa'i rnams//phyogs dang 'jig rten khams kyi rgyud du gyur pa dag/ji snyed yod pa der 'jug ste//brjod du med pa de dag rab tu shes//zhing dang zhing gi sa rnams kyi//brjod du med pa'i zhing rnams 
73a3(phal chen): kun kyang rab tu 'thob//de dag tshul yang rab tu shes//las rnams gang gis sbyangs pa'ang khong du chud//gang yang zhing rnams du ma dag/las rnams khrag khrig kun gyis kun nas nyon mongs par//'gro bas kun tu bsgrub 
73a4(phal chen): byas pa//de dag thams cad rgyud kyang ci 'dra shes//brjod du med pa yangs pa'i rnams//zhing gcig bstan pas de dag yongs su shes par gyur//de 'dra'i zhing la 'jug pa dang //gang la grangs med de dag kun la 
73a5(phal chen): 'jug/'jig rten khams ni gcig la yang //'jig rten khams rnams thams cad yang dag 'dzud pa byed//'jig rten gcig tu bsgongs mi 'gyur//'jig rten khams rnam de bzhin 
73a6(phal chen): 'dres mi 'gyur//spyi'u tshugs rnams dang gong ma'i rnams//bla dang 'og gis rnams yongs su gzung bya ste//'gro ba'i rnam par ming thogs pa//'jig rten kun la 'jug cing rab tu shes//snrel zhing rgyu ba'i rgyud rnams 
73a7(phal chen): dang //zhing rnams mtha' yas mtha' dang dbus yod ma yin pa//sna tshogs yangs pa rab tu shes//rnam pa gcig shes rnam pa tha dad shes//zhing gi grangs rnams rab tu shes//ye shes chags med mtha' ldan kun gyi grangs 
73a8(phal chen): rnams kyang //bzang po'i theg pa spyod pa yis//rgyal sras kun tu bzang rnams rab tu shes//'jig rten thams cad sprul pa dang //sems can rnams sprul dang de bzhin zhing sprul dang //chos rnams sprul pa'i tshul shes 
73b1(phal chen): pa||sangs rgyas sprul pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa'i rnams//'jig rten phra zhing zab pa dang //yangs shing sna tshogs yangs pas cho ma cho dgu'i rnams//sna tshogs tha dad rnams bsags pa//las kyi rnam smin gnas pa'i 
73b2(phal chen): 'jig rten rnams//rdzu 'phrul rnam par 'phrul pa yis//chos dbyings 'jug par bya la shin tu bslabs pa yi//rgyal sras mtha' dang dbus med pa//phyogs bcu thams cad ma lus 'gengs par byed//'jig rten khams 
73b3(phal chen): rnams de dag gi/ming ni bskal pa sems can snyed kyi mthar thug cing //zad par brjod par mi nus ste//ming bshad gal te sangs rgyas gsung na gsung //'jig rten mgon po rnams kyi yang //mtshan rnams mtshan nyid mi mthun 
73b4(phal chen): rnam pa sna tshogs mtshan//bskal pa brjod med zad byas kyang //mtshan rnams gcig nas gcig brgyud mthar thug med//'on kyang dma' ba'i sems med par//mkhas pa rnams ni dus gsum bde gshegs 
73b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas phyir//chos kyi dbyings la byung ba ste//sangs rgyas ye shes sa la'ang rab tu zhugs//dran pas chags med dag pa ste//chags med yid dang ldan zhing blo gros mtha' yas pas//
73b6(phal chen): ye shes blo gros mchog ldan pa//chos dbyings bshad la pha rol phyin pa thob//gang gis 'jig rten khams kyi rnams//yangs dang phra ba sna tshogs shes gyur cing //der ni 'das pa'i dus rnams la//sems gcig 
73b7(phal chen): bskyed pas rab tu 'jug pa byed//mi yi seng ge de dag tu//sangs rgyas spyod pa sna tshogs rnam pas chags med par//byang chub mchog tu sangs rgyas shing //rnam par 'phrul pa thams cad ston pa mdzad//de bzhin ma 'ongs dus 
73b8(phal chen): rnams kyi//rjes su gang byon de'i bskal pa ci 'dra dang //de la mi yi seng ge gang //skye bar 'gyur ba de dag kun kyang shes//smon lam spyod pa ci 'dra dang //spyod yul gang rnams bsgom pa gang gis grub gyur cing //bsgrub pa'i 
74a1(phal chen): @#//rnam pa ci 'dra bas//bskal pa gang la byang chub 'tshang rgya dang //de dag 'khor kyang khong du chud//sku tshe'i tshad dang sems can 'dul ba ci 'dra dang //chos kyi sgo mo gang zhig gis//'gro bar chos 
74a2(phal chen): kyi 'khor lo bskor ba shes//de ltar kun tu bzang po yi//ye shes mchog gis bsgrubs pa'i spyod pa'i sa gnas shing //de dag thams cad rab tu shes//de ni 'gro gtso kun gyi 'byung gnas yin//de ltar byung ba gzung 
74a3(phal chen): byas shing //'jig rten khams rnams thams cad 'dab chags ma lus par//'jig rten khams la rab tu 'jug/de bzhin chos kyis dbyings rnams rab tu shes//des ni mi yi gtso bo rnams//dgra las rgyal zhing 
74a4(phal chen): thams cad gzigs pa ji snyed pa//mi thub rgol ba dpa' bo'i rnams//chos kyi mnga' la pha rol phyin brnyes pa//de dag 'khor kyang rab tu shes//sems can 'dul dang zhing rnams rnam par dag pa dang //
74a5(phal chen): rgyal ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba'i rnams//sems pas bskal pa yangs pa zad par byed//skyes bu'i mchog rnams 'dul mdzad cinga //'jig rten mchog tu phyin pa'i byin rlabs dam pa la//mi zad 
74a6(phal chen): mdzod kyi shes yangs pas//ye shes tshul la mkhas pa rab tu 'jug/chags med mig gis blta ba dang //chags med rna ba mngon par bsgrub pa sgrub byed cing //chags pa med pa'i lus yangs dang //yangs pa'i lces kyang 
74a7(phal chen): 'jig rten tshim par byed//dam pa'i sems la chags med de//bsam pa kun tu rnam yangs rnam par dag dang ldan//ye shes 'byung gnas bdag nyid che//dus gsum sems rnams ma lus des khyab po//sprul pa'i pha rol 
74a8(phal chen): phyin thob cing //zhing rnams sprul pa'i bslab par bya ba bslab pa'i rnams//sems can sprul la mkhas pa dag/sprul pa sprul pas 'jig rten 'dul ba byed//'du shes rnam pa du ma ste//des ni 'jig rten sna tshogs ma lus 
74b1(phal chen): rnam par bris//de dag thams cad rab tu shes//sangs rgyas ye shes tshul la rab tu 'jug/'khor rnams brjod du med pa dag/sangs rgyas sku rnams re re dag la yod pa'i rnams//kun la 'jug cing mthong bar byed//sems 
74b2(phal chen): can rnam par 'dul ba'i mtha' yang med//ye shes zab mo mthong bas na//ye shes nyi ma shin tu gsal ba shar gyur pa'i//phyir mi ldog pa'i spyod pa la//'jig rten 'byam klas kun tu 'jug par byed//ji lta'i tshul du zhugs
74b3(phal chen): gyur cing //'jig rten 'di dag thams cad rnam par bkod 'dra bar//sems can tshul bzhin rab tu 'phags//khams gsum thams cad mig yor tshul 'drar shes//'on kyang rtog dang rnam rtog med//'jig rten 
74b4(phal chen): mtha' dang dbung med kun la rtog mi byed//rtog dang rtog med mkhas pa ste//rtog pa'i shes pas rnam par rtog pa med//bskal pa brjod du med pa'i rnams//sems bskyed gcig la yang dag 'du bar 
74b5(phal chen): mdzud byed cing //'jig rten mi khom 'di mthong nas//skad cig sems kyis thams cad shes par byed//brjod du med pa'i zhing rnams kyang //sems bskyed gcig gis thams cad yongasu 'da'
74b6(phal chen): bar byed//bskal pa brjod med zad byed kyang //skra'i rtse'i cha shas gcig las g.yo ba med//bskal pa brjod du mad pa dag/nyi ma gcig dang yud tsam gcig tu 'dzud byed kyang //ring dang thung la gzhol ba med//
74b7(phal chen): skad cig gcig la 'jug pa'i pha rol phyin//sems kyi rnam pa 'jig rten kun//'jig rten rnam pa sems kun gyi sems yin te//rnam pa gnyis kyi grangs su zhugs//'on kyang de dag mi gnyis rtog pa med//sgyu ma'i 
74b8(phal chen): rnam pa 'jig rten te//sems can zhing dang sangs rgyas chos kyang de bzhin no//'on kyang 'jig rten mgon po'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun la gcig tu snyoms//lus rnams brjod du med pa yis//phyogs 
75a1(phal chen): @#//bcu thams cad rgyas par bkang zhing yongs su ston//'on kyang lus la gzhol ba med//chos nyid rten pa gnas med rab tu shes//'on kyang mi yi seng ge rnams//gnyis la gnas pa'i shes 
75a2(phal chen): pas byung ba ma yin te//mi gnyis pa la'ang gnas pa med//gnyis dang gnyis med chos rnams rab tu shes//ji ltar smig rgyu rab rib dang //mig yor sgra brnyan de bzhin rmi lam 'dra ba dang //rmi lam rnam pa sprul pa ltar//
75a3(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad ma lus rab tu shes//de ltar spyod yul nyams med pas//sangs rgyas kun gyi rigs kyi rjes su 'byung ba yin//kun tu bzang po'i ye shes dang //chos kyi dbyings la shin tu bdal ba yin//snying rje chen po 
75a4(phal chen): mtha' yas kyang //sems can med dang mnyam par zhing rnams thams cad sgom//sngon nas nyon mongs nyams med pa'i//bsam pa dag pas 'jig rten kun tu 'gro//mi zad mkha' ltar rnam dag 
75a5(phal chen): pa'i||rgya chen shin tu yangs pa'i sa la rab tu zhugs//gsung ba'i seng ge thams cad kyi//tshul gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad de rjes su dran//'jig rten rab tu spros pa la//smon lam tshad med yongs 
75a6(phal chen): su gdab pa byas pas na//de dag spyod pa yongs su dag/'jig rten khams rnams thams cad rgyas par khyab//sangs rgyas rnams dang rgyas sras dang //de bzhin sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos rnams thams cad dang //'jig 
75a7(phal chen): rten rnams dang 'jig rten chos//chags med tha dad med pa'i tshul du shes//chos kyi sku'i snying po can//de'i lus la 'jig rten thams cad gzung byas shing //'jig rten de yi lus yin yang //'jig rten kun gyis de la 
75a8(phal chen): gos pa med//ji ltar dang pa'i chu gsal la//gzugs brnyan yongs su snang yang der song 'phos pa med//de bzhin 'jig rten thams cad kyi//rjes su lus 'gro der song 'phos pa med //de ltar 'jig rten thams cad kyis//
75b1(phal chen): gos pa med cing 'jig rten yongasu dag pas na//lus ni mkha' dang 'dra ba ste//de'i las rnams gang yang skyes pa med//mi zad pa yi lus shes pas//de ni nam yang skyes shing 'gags pa med pa ste//rtag dang mi rtag ma yin
75b2(phal chen): par//'jig rten kun la'ang rnam par dmigs pa byed//lta ba'i rnam pa rab tu 'gags//brtan pa de dag yang dag blta ba ston pa byed//bdag gi med cing gnas pa med//len pa med cing chos kyi dbyings dang mnyam//ji ltar
75b3(phal chen): sgyu mkhan mkhas pa zhig/sna tshogs mtshan nyid rnam pa tha dad gzugs 'phrul byed//'on kyang de la 'gro ba med//gang du 'gro ba de bzhin mi dmigs so//sgyu ma tshad med ma yin te//sgyu ma tshad kyis dmigs par bya ba'ang
75b4(phal chen): ma yin no//tshad med tshad dang ldan pa yang//tshogs kyi dbus su ston pa rab tu byed//de bzhin rtogs pa'i blo ldan pa//sangs rgyas kun gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams las byung //sems ni tshad yod ma yin te//
75b5(phal chen): tshad med pa la gnas pa'ang de dag min//'du shes rnam pa gcig min te//de dag tshad med pa dang tshad dang ldan pa ston//lam gyi rgyud dang 'brel ba med//tshad med rnams gnas pa'ang de bzhin med//
75b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas chos rnams zab pa ste//shin tu yangs pas zab cing zhi bas de bzhin zab//zab mo sna tshogs rgyud rnams dang //mtha' yas zab pa rnams kyang rab tu shes//ye shes bzang po rmongs pa med//rgyud la dri ma med
75b7(phal chen): pas bsam pa rnam par dag/byin gyis rlob la mkhas pas na//srog chags bye ba shin tu dag par byed//dge bar byed pa'i dkyil 'khor rnams//rab tu brjod cing dge dang mi dge rnam par ston//gnas la mngon par
75b8(phal chen): ma chags pas//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun tu rab tu rgyu//sems can med mthar phyin gyur kyang//'jig rten thams cad ma lus de dag khong du chud//'jig rten thams cad malus par//rnam par sangs rgyas mya ngan gar
76a1(phal chen): @#//mi 'da'||chos rnams bgrang pa'i tshul la mkhas//sems can bgrang tshul shes kyang grangs la skyes pa med//chos kyi sprin chen char 'bebs shing //'jig rten khams rnam+sa 
76a2(phal chen): thams cad snang bar byed//sangs rgyas byang chub ma lus la//sems bskyed gcig gis de dag rab tu 'jug/de dag phyir ldog med pa ste//sems can kun gyi khams las phyir mi ldog/sangs rgyas sku ni thob 
76a3(phal chen): gyur kyang //chos rnams 'di dag lus yod ma yin rab tu shes//lus rnams kun gyi tshul la mkhas//lus kyis rnam phye 'jig rten rab tu shes//sems can kun tu rgyu ba dang //bskal pa rnams dang zhing la pha rol
76a4(phal chen): phyin pa thob//phyogs rnams rgya mtsho gangs chen mtshor//ye shes rgya mtsho'i tshul la mkhas pas zhugs//lus rnams thams cad re re las//sems can kun gyi lus rnams sna tshogs ji snyed 
76a5(phal chen): dang|sangs rgyas sku ni mtha' yas pa//ye shes shin tu yangs pa de ston to//skad gcig gcig gi dus tshul la//mi'i gtso bo mang po 'byung ba rab tu shes//bskal pa mang po brjod med 
76a6(phal chen): du|mi yi seng ge bgrang bas zad mi 'gyur//de dag mya ngan 'da' ba yang //skad cig gcig la tshad med shin tu yangs pa 'byung //ring bsrel rnam dbye mang po yang //ye shes dri med phra bas ston pa byed//de bzhin 
76a7(phal chen): ma 'ongs dus kyi yang //mi'i seng ge mtha' yas smon lam 'debs pa'i rnams//byang chub dri med sems bskyed pa//ye shes yangs shing shin tu phra bas shes//bzang po spyod la gnas bcas shing //de bzhin
76a8(phal chen): dus gsum gshegs pa'i sems can gtso bo rnams//ji snyed skyes par gyur pa dag/de dag thams cad ma lus rab tu shes//de ltar rab tu shes pas na//mtha' dang dbung med spyod pa'i sa dang de bzhin du//
76b1(phal chen): ye shes 'byung gnas blo gros la//ye shes 'khor lo'i spyod pas phyir mi ldog /ye shes phra mo rab yangs pa//stobs bcu rnams kyi spyod yul mchog la de dag zhugs//bzang po rtogs pa'i blo can rnams//phyir 
76b2(phal chen): mi ldog la 'jug pa brjod bya na//sangs rgyas rgyal ba thams cad kyi//yang dag gzhol bar bya la de dag zhugs pas na//spyod las phyir ldog med pa ste//byang chub dam pa zhi bar 'tshang yang rgya//de 'dra'i sems 
76b3(phal chen): rgyud gcig la yang //mtha' dang dbung med sems rnams yang dag 'dzud pa byed//shin tu yangs pa'i tshad med las//'du shes de dang de la yang dag bsgrubs//nyon mongs med dang nyon mongs dang //slob dang mi 
76b4(phal chen): slob sems kyang de bzhin rab tu shes//sems rgyud de dag re re la ya da//sems rnams brjod du med pa mngon 'tshang rgya//gcig dang gnyis su shes pa med//de dag yongs su 'dres par 
76b5(phal chen): 'gyur ba'ang med pa ste//so so'i 'du shes rnam pa la//sems can de dag skyes par gyur pa yin//'di na 'dug par mthong gyur kya nga //sems can brjod med gzhan dang gzhan sems ldan 
76b6(phal chen): pa'i rnams//gzhan gyi zhing phyin 'du shes kyang //'jig rten sna tshogs rnams la rab tu skye//'di 'dra'i tshul la 'jug pa la//spyod pa'i mchog gis rjes su zhugs par gyur pa'i rnams//kun tu bzang po'i 
76b7(phal chen): ming thob ste//'jig rten mgon po kun gyi rjes su skyes//sems can bye ba la la ni//ngan song 'grangs pa'i ming thogs mtho ris 'du shes 'gyur//la la mtho ris sa rnams kyi//rnam pa kun la ngan song 
76b8(phal chen): 'du shes 'gyur//'jig rten mi mtshungs skal la ltos//rnam pa tha dad las kyi sa las rab tu skyes//'du shes rnam pa mtha' yas ste//gcig min yangs la 'jig rten rab tu 'jug /'jig rten 
77a1(phal chen): @#//rten khams rnams thams cad kyi//'du shes dra ba skal pa mi mnyam rab tu shes//mig 'phrul sgyu ma'i tshul gyis na//skad cig gcig gis sems kyis rab tu gzhol//
77a2(phal chen): de bzhin dbang po rnams la ste//mig gi skye mched rna ba sna'i skye mched dang //lce dang lus dang yid la yang //'jig rten rnam pa tha dad 'du shes 'jug/mig rnams re re'i yul la yang //mtha' dang dbung med mig 
77a3(phal chen): rnams rab tu 'dzud pa byed//rnam pa tha dad du ma ste//tshad med kun la'ang zhen par 'gyur ba med//'on kyang phan tshun gcig gis gcig/bdo zhing gnod par 'gyur ba de dag kun na med//bdag bdag so so'i las kyi rnams//
77a4(phal chen): ji ltar bsags pa'i tshul bzhin nyamasu myong //bzang zhes bya ba de dag kun//tshad med stobs dang ye shes sa'i shes rab kyis//'byam klas mig rnams thams cad la//rab tu zhugs pas shin tu rtogs pa yin//
77a5(phal chen): de bzhin 'jig rten thams cad kyang //thams cad ma lus rnam pa thams cad 'jig tshul du//thams cad shin tu shes gyur kyang //rgya chen spyod pas mya ngan mi 'da'o//sangs rgyas shin tu bstan pa dang //
77a6(phal chen): sems can bstan dang de bzhin zhing rnams bstan pa dang //dus rnams shin tu bstan pa dang bstan pa sna tshogs rnam pa kun kyang shes//la la 'di la 'das gyur kyang //gzhan dang gzhan rnams ma 'ongs dus la gnas pa 
77a7(phal chen): yin//'on kyang ma 'ongs dus kyi rnams//la la sa da ltar byung bar rab tu shes//de 'dra'i tshul can gcig min pa'i//'jig rten mtshan nyid tha dad thams cad rab tu shes//thams cad mkhyen pa nyid tshul gyis//sangs rgyas ye

93a5(phal chen): bya'o||kye rgyal sras 'di lta ste dper na/nam mkha'i dbyings ni thams cad kyi rjes su song ba ste/'dod pa dang gzugs yod pa dang /gzugs med pa'i gnas thams cad khyab pa yin yang /
93a6(phal chen): gang du 'ang song ba med cing /rjesu song ba med par shes par bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni nam mkha' la gzugs med pa'i phyir ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku yang thams cad kyi rjes su song

93b5(phal chen): lta ste dper na/'dzam bu'i gling du nyi ma shar ba ni 'od kyis tshad med par khyab pas/sems can rnams tshad med pa'i rnam pas nye bar 'tsho ste/'di ltar ri la bab cing rtswa dang nags tshal skye bar byed 
93b6(phal chen): pas kyang nye bar 'tsho ba yin mun pa'i mun nag yongs su sel cing /snang bar byed pas kyang nye bar 'tsho ba yin /sa la bab cing 'dam rdzab yongasu skems pas kyang nye bar 'tsho bayin /lo tog la bab cing yongasu smin par 
93b7(phal chen): byed pas kyang nye bar 'tsho ba yin nags tshal dang gel ba dang /sman la bab cing skye ba dang /rnam par 'phel bar byed pas kyang nye bar 'tsho ba yin /nam mkha' la bab kyang bar snang la spyod pa'i sems can rnams nye bar 'tsho ba yin/
93b8(phal chen): pad+mo'i nags tshal la bab kyang kha 'bye zhing rgyas par byed pas nye bar 'tsho ba yin/grong dang grong khyer dang ljongs dang pho brang 'khor dang /rgyal po'i gnas la bab kyang /gzugs sna tshogs gsal bar ston pas nye bar 
94a1(phal chen): @#//'tsho ba yin/chu la bab kyang chu la gnas pa'i sems can yongasu longs spyod pas nye bar 'tsho ba yin/las kyi rnam pa sna tshogs la bab kyang dgos pa brtsams pa yongs su grub par byed pas 
94a2(phal chen): nye bar 'tsho ba yin/de ci'i phyir zhe na/de ni nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor gyi 'od zer tshad med pa bkye ba'i phyir ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bltar yod pa'i nyi ma yang /rnam pa tshad med pas sems can 
94a3(phal chen): thams cad nye bar 'tsho ba yin te/'di ltar dge ba'i las yongasu skyed cing sdig pa'i las rnam par zlog pas kyang nye bar 'tsho ba yin/shes rab kyi 'od snang ba mdzad cing gti mug gi mun pa rnam par sel pas nye bar 'tsho ba yin 
94a4(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad yongasu skyob cing /byams pa chen po dang ldan pas kyang nye bar 'tsho ba yan/'gro ba thams cad yongasu 'grel cing /thugs rje chen po dang ldan pas kyanganye bar 'tsho ba yin/dbang po dang /

94b4(phal chen): la|'dir ni thog ma nyidu 'od zer dgye'o zhe 'am/'dir ni thog mar snang bar bya'o zhes bya ba'i rnam par rtog pa med de/spros pa med kyang nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor 'od mnyam du gtong zhing 'char ba ni med pa ma yin

95b4(phal chen): mig nang dang /lus la gnas pa'i nad/rlung 'am mkhris pa 'am/bad kan las byung ba de dag thams cad sel bar byed pa'i sgor mi phan pa ma yin te/phan 'dogs so//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du/

95b8(phal chen): ba dang /ma 'ongs pa'i nyon mongs pa'i rgyu'i lhags pa sel ba'i sgor de bzhin gshegs pa'i yi shes kyi sku nyi ma'i dkyil pa 'khor phan par mi 'gyur ba ma yin te phan 'dogs so//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin gshegs 
96a1(phal chen): @#//pa la yon tan rin po che thams cad yang dag par bsags pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi snang ba dang ldan pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zer rang /rnam par dag pas 
96a2(phal chen): rnam par rol pa'i ye shes kyi snang ba ldan pa zhes bya ba'i 'od zar dang /dbyangs chen pos ye shes rnam par dgod pa'i don snang ba dang ldan pa zhes ba ba'i 'od zer dang /spyod pa thams cad kyis shin tu tshim par byed pa'i

96a7(phal chen): zer de dag mthong bar gyur na/byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing gzhan dang gzhan na 'khod pa rnams kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa mtshan tha dad pa rnams rab tu shes par 'gyur te/byang chub sems dpa' de dag 
96a8(phal chen): thams cad kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul la mgo yongs su dag pa bcu rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul mig yongs su dag pa bcu dang /rna ba yongs su dag pa bcu dag /sna yongs su dag 
96b1(phal chen): pa bcu dang /lce yongs su dag pa bcu dang /lus yongs su dag pa bcu dang/lag pa yongs su dag pa bcu dang/rkang pa yongs su dag pa bcu dang /sa yongs su dag pa bcu danga /ye shes 'byam klas pa yongs su dag pa

97b1(phal chen): ba'i tshe rmad du byung ba'i chos bzhi snga phyi med par byed de bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste/skar ma thams cad zil gyis gnon te/'phel ba dang /'grib pa yang yongs su ston to//'dzam bu'i gling thams cad kyi chu dang 
97b2(phal chen): ba'i snod kun tu 'ang gzugs brnyan rab tu snang ngo//sems can kun gyis bltas na'ang mngon sum du gnas par snang ste/de la rnam par rtog pa med do//sprul pa med do//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku zla

97b7(phal chen): bar 'gyur te/sems can ji lta bur 'dul ba bzhin du snang ba'i tshul gyis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'ang rab tu ston te/de bzhin gshegasapa'i sku zla ba'i dkyil 'khor de la rnam par rtog pa mi mnga'/spros pa mi mnga'o//

98a1(phal chen): @#//sku bltar yod pa shes par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang 'di lta ste dper na/stong gsum gyi ston chen po'i bdag po tshangs pa chen po ni/las gron chung dus stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 
98a2(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du lus yongs su ston te/'on kyang lus yongs su 'byed pa med do//lus tha dad par byed pa med do//stong gsum gyi stong chen por gtogs pa'i sems can rnams kyis kyang /bsam pa mnyam pa nyid 
98a3(phal chen): ji lta bu dang ldan pa'i tshul bzhin du/lus mnyam pa nyid du mthong ngo//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa yang /sku yongs su 'byed pa mi mdzad/sku tha dad par mdzad pa yang mi mnga' bar 'jig rten gyi khams 
98a4(phal chen): thams cad du gtogs pa'i sems can rnal la yang bsam pa dang /mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du/sku yongs su ston te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku la rnam par rtog pa mi mnga'/spros pa mi mnga'o//kye rgyal ba'i 
98a5(phal chen): sras byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos khong du chud par bya ba'i sgo bdun pa 'dis kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i skub ltar yod par shes par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang 'di 
98a6(phal chen): lta ste dper na/sman pa'i rgyal po chen po sman gyi yod byad dang /cho ga la shin tu mkhas pa btsug lag dang sngags thams cad kyi tshul shes pa zhig yod la 'dzam bu'i gling gi sman thams cad de'i dbang du gyur te/sman pa'i rgyal 
98a7(phal chen): po chen po de'i sngon gyi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i stobs kyi shugs dang /sngags dang /rigs sngags kyi mthu dang /thabs kyis mthong ma thag tu sems can rnams kyi nad yongs su 'tsho bar 'gyur zhing /nad med par 'gyur ba zhig yod de/
98a8(phal chen): de nas sman pa'i rgyal po de 'chi ba'i dus la bab par gyur na/de 'di snyam du sems par gyur te/kyi hud sems can 'di dag bdag shi ba dang mgon med par 'gyur gyis/bdag gi thabs mkhas pa'i stobs kyis sman thams 
98b1(phal chen): cad yongasu bsdus te/bdag gi lus sku'o//sngags dang rigs sngags kyi stobs dang /byin gyi brlabs kyis kyang /bdag gi lus byin gyis brlabs te/ci nas kyang bdag shi nas lus mi 'jig pa dang /mi skam pa dang /
98b2(phal chen): mi nyams shing 'gro ba dang 'ong ba dang /'dug pas kyang spyod pa dang /nad thams cad sos par bya ba'i phyir/sman pa'i las su bya ba thams cad kyang spyod cing blta ba dang /mnyan pa'i don spyod pas kyang /sngon ji lta ba bzhin du 
98b3(phal chen): phyin chad kyang gnas par bya'o snyam du sems pa zhig yod na/kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas sman pa'i rgyal po chen po bla na med pa nyon mongs pa'i nad 
98b4(phal chen): thams cad rab tu zhi bar mdzad pa la mkhas pa/bskal pa bye ba khrag khri ga brgya stong mang pos kun mkhyen pa'i rtsil/brtson 'grus shin tu mdzad pa/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i thabs mkhas pa dang /sngags 
98b5(phal chen): dang rigs sngags la shin tu bslabs pa/stobs dang mi bsnyengs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa la rab tu mkhas pa sems can thams cad yongasu smin par bya ba dang /gdul ba'i tshul 
98b6(phal chen): mkhyen pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi mnga' chen po brnyes pa des kyang /sngon byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la bzhugs shing /bsod nams dang /ye shes kyi tshogs dang /shes rab kyi pha rol du phyin pa'i thabs 
98b7(phal chen): mkhas pa'i sngags dang /rigs sngags dang /sman gyi mthus phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i bskal par 'gro ba thams cad kyi nyon mongs pa'i nad gso ba'i phyir/sku tshe'i tshad byin gyis brlabs pas/de'i sku yongs su dag 
98b8(phal chen): pa'i phyir/mthong ma thag tu ched du ma mdzad cing lhun gyis grub par/sems can rnams kyi nyon mongs pa'i nad thams cad rab tu zhi bar 'gyur te/sangs rgyas kyi phrin las thams cad kyang /rgyun mi 'chad par 'byung ngo//
99a1(phal chen): @#//kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos khong du chud par bya ba'i sgo brgyad pa 'dis kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bltar yod pa shes par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan 
99a2(phal chen): yang 'di lta ste dper na/rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na/nor bu rin po che chen po 'od thams cad 'dus pas rnam par snang ba'i snying po zhes bya ba yod de/kye rgyal ba'i sras nor bu rin po che chen po'i 'od kyis/sems can gang la bab 
99a3(phal chen): pa de dag thams cad kyang 'di lta ste/nor bu rin po che 'od thams cad 'dus pa/rnam par snang ba'i snying po'i kha dog du kha dog gcig par 'gyur ro//gang gis nor bu rin po che de mthong bar gyur pa de dag gi mig kyang yongs 
99a4(phal chen): su dag par 'gyur ro//nor bu rin po che de'i 'od kyi dkyil 'khor gyis ji tsam du khyab par gyur pa de tsam du 'od kyi dkyil 'khor de las sems can bde bar byed pa zhes bya ba'i nor bu rin po che'i char 
99a5(phal chen): 'bab par 'gyur te/des sems can de dag kyang rab tu bde bar 'gyur la/sdug bsngal yang rgyun 'chad par 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba 'i sras ltos dang /nor bu rin po che de chos ci la yang mi rtog na/
99a6(phal chen): 'on kyang don chen po rab tu sgrub po//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du/de bzhin gshegs pa la yang sku rin po che chen po bsod nams thams cad yongs su bsdus pas/ye shes chen po'i snying po dang ldan pa mnga' ste/de bzhin 
99a7(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sku rin po che'i ye shes kyi 'od zer de dag gis/sems can ji snyed la reg par gyur pa de dag thams cad kyang /'di lta ste de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku'i kha dog tu kha dog gcig par 'gyur ro//sems 
99a8(phal chen): can gang gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku mthong bar gyur pa de dag gi chos kyi mig kyang rnam par dag par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rin po che 'i 'od gyi dra ba'i dkyil 'khor gyis sems can ci snyed la reg 
99b1(phal chen): pa de dag thams cad kyang /dbul ba dang yongasu bral bar 'gyur te/byang chub kyi sgo'i bar gyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i bde ba yang rab tu 'thob par 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras ltos dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i
99b2(phal chen): sku chos gang la yang rtog pa mi mnga' ste/spros pa mi mnga' yang 'gro ba thams cad kyi don yongs su thob par mdzad do//kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos khong du chud par bya ba'i sgo dgu
99b3(phal chen): pa 'dis kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bltar yod pa shes par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i gzhan yang 'di lta ste dper na/rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na/rin po che'i rgyal po chen po yid bzhin gyi nor bu/'gro ba thams cad kyi
99b4(phal chen): rgyan gyi snying po zhes bya ba yod de/rin po che'i rgyal po de ni yon tan brgya stong phrag bcu dang ldan pa ste/nor bu rin po che'i rgyal po de gang na 'dug pa de 'khor gyi sems can kun gyi nad dang /sdug
99b5(phal chen): bsngal gyi gtse ba thams cad med par 'gyur ro//sems can ji snyed cig nor bu rin po che'i rgyal bo de la/'dod pas sems pa de dag thams cad kyi bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par byed de/rin po che'i
99b6(phal chen): rgyal po chen po de ni bsod nams ma byas pa'i sems can rnams kyi/mig lam du snang bar 'gyur ba ma yin no//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la nor bu rin po che'i rgyal pa chen po zhes bya ba ni/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku sems
99b7(phal chen): can thams cad shin tu tshim par mdzad pa'i tshig bla dgas te/gang na de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bltar yod cing mtshan mnyan du yod la yon tan dang /legs pa yongs su brjodu yod pa de phyogs kyi sems can kun gyi
99b8(phal chen): 'khor ba la gnas pa'i sdug bsngal dang /mi bde ba thams cad med par gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku rin po che de ni/ci ste sems can 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du gtogs
100a1(phal chen): @#//pa kun gyis snga phyi med par sems rtse gcig du yid la bya ba ma 'khrug pas/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku blta ba'i phyir/dmigs pa byas na de dag thams cad kyis/so so nas de bzhin gsheDa
100a2(phal chen): pa'i sku'ang mthong bar 'gyur la/thams cad kyi chos kyi bsam ba yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ro//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku de yang /sems can 'dul ba'i dbang du de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyi brlabs kyis
100a3(phal chen): bstan pa ma gtogs par sems can bsod nams ma byas pa rnams kyi mig lam du snang bar mi 'gyur te/sems can su la'ang rung ste/dge ba'i rtsa ba de bzhin gshegs pa'i skubastan pas yongasu smin par bya ba zhig yod 
100a4(phal chen): na|de dag yongasu smin par bya ba'i don du de bzhin gshegs pa sku rin po che ston to//kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po sems tshad med pa'i rjesu song zhing /phyogs kyi dra ba thaMd 
100a5(phal chen): rgyas par 'gengs pa/namakha' lta bur chags pa med pa'i spyod yul dang ldan pa/chos kyi dbyings mnyam pa nyid khong du chud pa yang dag pa'i mtha' mnyam pa nyid la gnas pa de bzhin nyid la gnas 
100a6(phal chen): pa de bzhin nyid ma skyes ma 'gags pa dang /dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi rjesu song ba rtog pa dang /rnam par rtog pa dang /yongasu rtog pa thams cad dang bral ba phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug pa'i lhag pa'i bsam pa la zhugs 
100a7(phal chen): pa|'jig rten gyi khams dang /chos kyi dbyings ma lus pa thams cad du khyab pa/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad dang /sku'i rgyun gcig tu gyur pas/khong du chud par bya ba'i sgo 'dis kyang /de bzhin gshegs 
100a8(phal chen): pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi sku bltar yod pa shes par bya'o//de nas byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po kun tu bzang pos/don 'di nyid yang brtag par bya ba'i phyir/de'i tshe 
100b1(phal chen): tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smras so//ji ltar mkha' dbyings kun tu mu med dbyings yin te//gzugs dang gzugs med de bzhin dngos dang dngos med kun//phyogs dang phyogs mtshams kun dang sems can lus dang 
100b2(phal chen): zhing //dus gsum ma lus mkha' dbyings des khyab po//de bzhin gshegs sku kun nas bltar yod de bzhin te//'jig rten khams dang chos dbyings ma khyab gang yang med//rnam pa kun tu bltar med gzung med rgyal ba'i 
100b3(phal chen): sku||'dul ba'i don yod mgon po'i sku ni bltar med min//ji ltar mkha' dbyings 'di ni gzung med dbyings yin pas//'gro ba kun gyi las kyi rnam pa ma lus gnas//'on kyang bdag gis gang du ji ltar su yi don//
100b4(phal chen): ci dang ci zhig bya zhes mkha' dbyings rtog pa med//de bzhin skyes mchog sku'i phrin las brten bya ste//phyogs bcu kun nas dkar po'i chos rnams ci yod pa//sna tshogs mi rnams byed kyang sangs rgyas 
100b5(phal chen): sku la ni//'di'i don cig bya zhes yongs su rtog mi mnga'//ji ltar 'dzam bu'i gling du nyi ma 'char 'gyur tshe//mun pa ma lus sel cing rab tu snang ba byed//shing dang pad+mo'i nags tshal rnams gar 
100b6(phal chen): bab pa yi//sna tshogs gnas kyis sems can thams cad nye bar 'tsho//de bzhin rgyal ba'i nyi ma sems can kun 'tsho ste//lha mi 'jig rten rnams kyi 'dus byas dge rtsa la//mun pa'i thibs po bsal cing shes rab 
100b7(phal chen): snang 'gyur bas//mi'i dbang po mchog mthong bde ba thams cad 'thob//nyi ma'i 'od zer thog ma ri rab khor yug la//shar nas de'i 'og tu ri bo mang la 'char//de nas sa stengs 'di'i phyogs rnams mtho mtho la//
100b8(phal chen): de nas sa stengs dma' ba'i phyogs rnams snang bar byed//bde gshegs 'od zer thog mar rgyal sras lus la 'char /de'i 'og tu rang rgyal theg la gnas par 'char//de nas dbang sgyur de nas slob pa'i rnams 
101a1(phal chen): @#//la 'char//de nas ma lus 'gro 'char sangs rgyas rtog mi mnga'//mig dang mi ldan nyi ma kun tu snang mi mthong //'on kyang nyi ma'i grangs kyis de dag nye bar 'tsho//bkres pa spong zhing 
101a2(phal chen): lus la shin tu lham par byed//de ltar sems can thams cad nyi mas nye bar 'tsho//dad dang mi ldan sangs rgyas nyi ma mi mthong ste//'on kyang rgyal ba'i byin gyis de yang nye bar 'tsho//rgyal ba'i mtshan thos bde gshegs
101a3(phal chen): 'od la reg gyur kyang //de la byang chub go 'phang bar gyi rgyur 'gyur ro//zla ba shar ba skar ma thams cad zil gyis gnon//'gro ba rnams la 'phel dang 'grib pa rab tu ston//chu mtsho kun la gzugs brnyan rab tu snang 
101a4(phal chen): bar byed//gang rnams chu la lta ba de la mngon par snang //rgyal ba'i zla ba shar ba nyan thos zil gyis gnon//'dul ba ji bzhin rang sku 'phel dang 'grib pa ston//lha dang mi'i sems la gzugs brnyan 
101a5(phal chen): rab tu snang //'gro ba kun gyis bdag bdag mdun na bzhugs pa ra shes//tshangs pa ji ltar tshangs pa'i gnas nas ma g.yos kyang //stong gsum tshangs gnas kun tu rang gi lus kyang ston//lha dang mi 
101a6(phal chen): rnams rang gi lus kyi dbang du byed//tshangs pa rang gi lus kyang tha dad byed pa min//de bzhin chos rnams kun la mnga' 'gyur ston pa yang //phyogs bcu kun tu sku gzugs mtha' yas ston par mdzad//dpe zla med pa rnams kyi sku 
101a7(phal chen): la dbye ba med//mi rnams kun kyang so so'i ston pa mngon 'gyur shes//ji ltar gtsug lag kun la mkhas pa'i sman pa zhig/gang gis mthong bar gyur pa de dag nad 'jig pa//gang tshe tshe zad 'chir 'gyur de tshe sman bsdus shing //
101a8(phal chen): rang gi lus la bskus te spyod pa thams cad ston//de ltar mi mchog dbang po mtshungs med sman pa'i rgyal//thabs dang shes rab ye shes kun la rab mkhas pa//sngon gyi spyod pa'i khyad kyis ston pa'i sku ston te//mthong zhing 'gro 
101b1(phal chen): ba'i nyon mongs ma lus nad kyang 'jil//ji ltar gangs chen mtsho la rin chen 'byung yod pa//'od kyi rnam pa thams cad kun tu 'gyed byed de//sems can gang la reg pa de dag de tshul 'gyur//gang gis mthong 'gyur
101b2(phal chen): de dag mig kyang rnam par dag/rgyal ba rin chen de bzhin shes rab 'od 'gyed de//sems can sus reg de dag sangs rgyas kha dog 'gyur//gang gis rgyal ba mthong ba de dag mig lnga 'thob par 'gyur//mun pa rdul
101b3(phal chen): bsal rgyal ba'i sa la gnas par 'gyur//ji ltar nor bu rin chen bsam ba kun ster ba//gang rnams ci 'dod de la de dag thams cad 'byin//de ni bsod nams gang gis byas pa des mthong ste//rin chen dam (pa)de la rnam
101b4(phal chen): rtog gang yang med//de bzhin bde gshegs sku mchog bsam pa kun skong ba//mthong nas smon lam su 'debs de dag bsam pa 'grub//snying ring dma' ba'i sems ldan 'gro bas thub mi mthong //bde gsheg+sa
101b5(phal chen): rnam rtog mi mnga' ser sna mi mnga'o////bam po bdun cu gnyis pa//de skad ces smras pa dang/byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po de bzhin gshegs pa'i
101b6(phal chen): rigasu byung ba'i dpal gyis/byang chub sems dpa' sem dpa' chen po kun tu bzang po la 'di skad ces smras so//kye rgyal ba'i sras ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' semasadapa' chen pos de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom
101b7(phal chen): pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi gsung brjod pa khong du chud par bya/de skad ces smras pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' semams dpa' chen po kun tu bzad pos//byang chub sems dpa' semIdpa' chen po

102a1(phal chen): @#//gsung brjod pa ni/dbyangs kyi dkyil 'khor tshad med pas rgyas shing khyab par mdzad pa'i phyir/kun tu khyab par shes par bya'o//chos bstan pa mngon par bsgrub pa'i phyir/sems can thams 
102a2(phal chen): cad kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du tshim pa mdzad par shes par bya'o//sems rab tu tshim par mdzad pa'i phyir/'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad mos pa ji lta ba bzhin du/dga' bar mdzad par shes par bya'o //dus las

102a8(phal chen): sgra chen po'i dbyangs chen pa thog ma rnam pa 'di lta bu 'byung ste/kye ma grogs po dag bsam gtan dang po ni 'dod pa dang /gnod sems med pas shin tu bde ba ste/'dod pa'i khams las yang dag par 'da' ba'i rgyur 
102b1(phal chen): 'gyur ro//sgra de thos nas sems can rnams bsam gtan dang po bsgrubs te/'dod pa'i khams las 'das nas/tshangs pa'i 'jig rten du skye'o//de nas chos nyid thob pas/sgra chen po'i dbyangs chen po gnyis pa

102b6(phal chen): kye ma grogs po dag bsam gtan bzhi pa 'di ni shin tu zhi ba ste/dge rgyas kyi lha las yang dag pa 'da' ba'i rgyur 'gyur ro //sgra de thos nas kyang sems cad rnams bsam gtan bzhi pa bsgrubs te/dge rgyas kyi 
102b7(phal chen): lha las 'das nas/'bras bu che ba'i lha dang skal pa mnyam pa skye'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar 'jig rten gyi khams 'jig par 'gyur ba'i tshe/chos nyid rab tu thob pas/sgra chen po'i dbyangs chen po de bzhi 'byung 
102b8(phal chen): bar 'gyur te/de la bdag po 'am brjod par byed pa gang yang med mod kyi/de ltar tshad med pa'i yon tan dang ldan pa'i sgra rnams 'byung bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras debazhin du de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsung la'ang 
103a1(phal chen): @#//bdag po gang yang med de/brjod par byed pa med do//'phro bar byed pa med do//len pa med do gtong pa med do 'on kyang de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos nyid thob pas/sgra chen po'i dbyangs chen po 
103a2(phal chen): bzhi 'byung bar 'gyur te/bzhi gang zhe na/'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos nyad thob pas sgra chen po gcig 'byung ba ni/kye ma 'dus byas 'di dag sdug bsngal ba ste/sems can dmyal ba ni sdug bsngal lo//byol 
103a3(phal chen): song gi skye gnas ni sdug bsngal lo//gshin rje'i 'jig rten ni sdug bsngal lo//bsod nams ma byas pa ni sdug bsngal lo//bdag gir 'dzin pa ni sdug bsngal te/sdig pa'i las spyod pa ni sdug bsngal gyis/lha dang mir 
103a4(phal chen): skye ba'i phyir dge ba'i rtsa ba skyed cing /'phags pa'i skye mched yongs su zung shig/mi khom par skye ba yongs su spong shig/sgra de thos nas kyang sems can rnams ma nor bar nan tan rab tu byed de/de dag ma nor bar

113b2(phal chen): par 'byed pa mi mdzad kyang bsam pa dang sbyor ba'i khyad par gyis de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye she snang ba'i khyad par du 'gyur ba'ang yod do//kye rgyal ba'i sras khong du chud par bya ba'i sgo gsum pa 'dis kyang /byang chub

113b4(phal chen): chen po'i nang na/nor bu rin po che chen po yon tan tshad med pa phun sum tshogs pa bzhi yod de/nor bu rin po che de dag gi gzi brjid dang /mthus rgya mtsho chen por rin po che thams cad 'byung ste/kye rgyal
113b5(phal chen): ba'i sras galte rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na/nor bu yin po che de bzhi med du zin na ni/rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na nor bu rin po che gcig kyang yod par mi 'gyur ro//bzhi po de dag kyang gang zhe na 'di
113b6(phal chen): lta ste rin po che thams cad yang dag par bsdud pa zhes bya ba'i nor bu rin po che chen po dang /mi zad pa'i mdzod ces bya ba'i nor bu rin po cha chen po dang /snying bla sa med pa zhes bya ba'i nor bu yin po che chen po dang /rgyan thaMd
113b7(phal chen): yang dag par sdud pa zhes bya ba'i nor ba rin po che chen po ste/nor bu rin po che tshad med pa'i yon tan phun sum tshogs pa dang ldan pa de bzhi yod do//kye rgyal ba'i sras nor bu rin po che chen pa bzhi po de dag ni bsod
113b8(phal chen): nams ngan ngon dang ldan pa'i lha ma yin nam/nam mkha' lding ngam klu 'am/de ma yin pa'i sems can chu la gnas pa'i mig gis mthong bar mi 'gyur ro//de ci'i phyir zhe na/nor bu rin po che chen po de bzhi ni/

114a2(phal chen): bkod pa dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na/ye shes kyi rin po che chen po bsod nams dang /

114a7(phal chen): dad kyang rnam pa tha dad du shin tu ston pa'i ye shes rin po che chem po dang /dus dang dus ma yin pa las mi 'da' zhing /dus la phebs par byed pa'i ye shes rin po che chon po ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ya shes rin po che chen
114a8(phal chen): po de bzhi yod do//kye rgyal ba'i sras dag ye shes rin po che chen mo de dag ni/sems can bsod nams ngan ngon dang ldan pa rnams kyi mig gis mthong bar mi 'gyur te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/ye shes rin po che chen po de bzhi ni/
114b1(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i chos kyi mdzod na 'khod pa'i phyir te/kye rgyal ba'i sras ye shes rin po che chen po de dag ni rnam pa kun tu bzang pa ste/phyogs bzhir rnam par bkod cing /byang chub sems dpa' nye bar 'tsho ba dang /
114b2(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i ye shes snang bas 'thob pa dag ste/phyogs bzhir byang chub sems dpa' yang dag par bsgrub pa'i phyir rnam par bkod pa dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras khong du chud par bya ba'i sgo bzhi pa 'dis kyang byang chub 
114b3(phal chen): sems dpa' sems dpa' chen pos de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi thugs skye ba khong du chud par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang 'di lta ste dper na/rgya mtsho 
114b4(phal chen): de nyid kyi 'og na/nor bu rin po che chen po drag shul gyi gzi brjid dang ldan pa mthu shin tu che ba bzhi yod de/nor bu rin po che chen pi de dag gi gzi brjid dang mthus rgya mtsho chen por blugs pa'i chu'i phung po 
114b5(phal chen): thams cad yongs su 'grib par 'gyur ro//de yod pas rgya mtsho chen po yang 'phel ba dang /'grib par mi 'gyur ro//nor bu rin po che chen po de bzhi ni rgya mtsho chen po'i 'og gi mtshams na gnas pa 
114b6(phal chen): ste bzhi gang zhe na /'di lta ste/nyi ma'i snying po zhes bya ba'i nor bu rin po che dang /rlan med pa zhes bya ba'i nor bu rin pa che dang /me'i 'od ces bya ba'i nor bu rin po  che dang /ma lus pa'i mthar thugs pa zhes bya ba'i nor bu 
114b7(phal chen): rin po che dag ste/nor bu rin po che chen mo de bzhi yod do//kye rgyal ba'i sras gal te/rgya mtsho chen po la nor bu rin po che chen po  de bzhi ne da du zin na ni/gling chen po bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyang /gcig 
114b8(phal chen): tu chus yongs su rgyas par 'gyur te/khor yug dang khor yug chen po'i ri las kyang 'das shing /chu'i phung pos srid pa'i rtse mo man chad du'ang gang bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras rgya mtsho chen por 'dus pa'i chu'i 
115a1(phal chen): @#//phung po de dag kyang nor bu rin po che nyi ma'i snying po'i 'od kyis phog na/chu'i rang bzhin spangs nas 'o ma dang zhor gyur te 'dug go//nor bu rin po che chen po rlan med pa'i 'od kyis phog na/
115a2(phal chen): 'o ma dang zho'i rang bzhin spangs nas/mar gyi snying khu lta bur 'gyuro//me'i 'od kyi nor bu rin po che chen po'i 'od kyis phog na /mar gyi snying khu lta bu'i rang bzhin spangs te/de lta bur 'bar bar 'gyuro//nor bu rin 
115a3(phal chen): po che chen po ma lus pa'i mthar thugs pa'i 'od kyis phog na/thams cad lhag ma med cing ma lus pa mi snang bar 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin du de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs 
115a4(phal chen): pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi ye shes kyi rgya mtsho chen po la/ye shes rin po che chen po mthu dang 'od shin tu che ba bzhi yod de/ye shes rin po che chen pa de bzhi'i mthu dang 'od kyi ma phog na/byang chub

115a6(phal chen): par mi mngon no//bzhi po de dag kyang gang zhe na/'di lta ste/dba' rlabs thams cad zhi bar byed pa'i ye shes rin po che chen po dang /chos kyi rjes su 'brang zhing sred pa yongs su dag par byed pa'i ye shes rin pa che chen 
115a7(phal chen): po dang /shes rab kyi 'od chen pos shin tu snang ba'i ye shes rin po che chen mo dang /mun pa med cing snang ba med pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i mnyam pa nyid kyi ye shes rin po che chen poste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye 
115a8(phal chen): shes rin po che mthu dang /gzi brjid shin tu che ba 'di bzhi yod do//kye rgyal ba'i sras de bzhin gshegs pa ye shes rin po che chen pa 'di bzhi'i gzi brjid dang mthus byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyang /
115b1(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes mnyam pa nyid 'thob par 'gyur ro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shed kyi rgya mtsho chen po de la/byang chub sems dpa' bsod nams dang /byang chub kyi 
115b2(phal chen): tshogs chen po bsags pa rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i dba' rlabs kyi shugs drag po lha dang mi dang lha ma yin gyi 'jig rten du bcas pas/mi zlogs shing bsam gyis mi khyab pa ldang ste/byang chub sems dpa'i dge 
115b3(phal chen): ba'i rtsa ba'i dba' rlabs ku shugs bsam gyis mi khyab pa de dag la de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shas rin po che chen po dba' rlabs thams cad zhi bar byed pa'i 'od kyi phog na/dge ba'i rtsa ba'i dba' rlab kyi shugs 
115b4(phal chen): drag po spangs nas ting nge 'dzin la rtse gcig tu gnas par 'gyur ro//chos kyi rjesu 'brang zhing sred pa zad par byed pa'i ye shes rin po che chen po'i 'od kyis phog na/ting nge 'dzin gyi ro dang /gnas rnam par 
115b5(phal chen): spangs te/mngon par shes pa rgya chen po mngon par bsgrubs pa'i tshul la gnas par 'gyur ro/shes rab kyi 'od chen pos shin tu snang ba'i ye shes rin po che chenapo'i 'od kyis phog na/mngon par 
115b6(phal chen): shes pa rgya chen po mngon bar bsgrub pa'i tshul rnam par spangs te/rig pa'i snang ba chen po mngon par bsgrub pa'i tshul la gnas par gyur ro//mun pa med cing snang ba med pa de bzhin gshegs pa'i mnyam ba nyid 
115b7(phal chen): kyi ye shes rin po che chen pa'i 'od kyis phog na/rig pa'i snang ba chen po 'don par bsgrub pa'i tshul rnam pa spangs te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa mnyam pa nyid ched du bya ba yongasu zhi ba phrin las lhun gyis grub par 
115b8(phal chen): 'byung ba rab tu thob bo//kye rgyal ba'i sras ye shes rin po che chen mo 'di bzhi'i 'od snang bas phog pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i mnya pa nyid bzhi po de dag 'thob par 'gyur ro//
116a1(phal chen): @#//kye rgyal ba'i sras gal te de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi rgya mtsho chen po'i nang na/ye shes rin po che chen po 'di bzhi med du zin na/chung ngu na byang chub sems dpa' gcig gis kyang 
116a2(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa mnyam pa nyid 'thob par 'gyur ba'i gnas med skabs med de/de ni gnas ma yin no//kye rgyal ba'i sras bod du chud par bya ba'i sgo lnga pa 'dis kyang /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang

116a4(phal chen): gyi skye mched kyi srid pa'i rtse mo'i bar gyi stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad namakha' la gnas pa ste/gzugs med pa la spyod pa'i sems can gyi gnas dang /gzugs 
116a5(phal chen): la spyod pa'i sems can gyis gnas dang /'dod pa la spyod pa'i sems can gyi gnas ji snyed pa de dag thams cad namakha' la gnad shing nam mkha'i dbyings las byung pa'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/

116b1(phal chen): pa'i ye shes kyi nam mkha'i dbyings kyis kun tu khyab pa'i phyir te/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes la gnod pa 'am/mi bde bar 'gyur ba med kyang /ye shes yang dag par 'grub pa'i rnam pa tha dad par 'gyur ba'ang medo//

117b1(phal chen): ga 'dzin to//lo ma'i 'od las tshul khrims dang bslab pa dang /sbyangs pa'i yon tan dang /sdom pa thams cad kyi lo ma 'dzin to//me tog gi 'od las mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po dge ba'i rtsa ba sna tshogs kyis grub pa'i me tog

117b8(phal chen): du chud par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang 'di lta ste dper na/mes 'jig pa'i bskal ba 'byung ba'i tshe/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams nyi ma bdun shar cing /tshig pa dang 'bar ba
118a1(phal chen): @#//dang /rab tu 'bar ba dang /me'i phung po chen por gyur te/gcig tu 'bar bar gyur nas/rtswa dang gel ba dang sman dang nags tshal yan chad/khor yug dang khor yug chen po'i 
118a2(phal chen): bar du 'tshig cing thal ba'ang mi mngon par 'gyur ba de'i tshe/mi la la zhig gis rtsa skam po drung ba rlan gyi bag med pa khur chen po gcig cig bkur bkur te bcug na/kye rgyal ba'i sras de la ji snyam du sems/

118a4(phal chen): po chen po de'i nang gi rtse'i zags ma 'ga' zhig ma tshig pa ni yod kyang srid kyi/de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyis ni dus gsum na yod pa'i sems can 'grangs pa dang zhing 'grangs pa dang /bskal pa

118a8(phal chen): dang khor yug chen po'i rdo rje'i ri yan chad stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad bshig bcom rnam par gtor te/cha shas kyang med par byed de/de la phyir zlog pa zhes bya ba'i rlung dmar po gzhan 
118b1(phal chen): ldang ba'ang yod de/de dag ni stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i 'jig rten gyi khams kyi 'khor kun nas ldang ste/rnam par 'thor ba'i rlung dmar chen po 'jig rten gyi khams gzhan du mi 'gro bar phyir zlog go/kye rgyal ba'i

167a1(phal chen): @#//byang chub sems dpa' byang chub sems dpa'i spyad pa spyod//pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad la 'jug pa la gnas shing /byang chub sems dpa' brgyad ba sems dpa'i spyad pa sbyod//

167b8(phal chen): dus na/gang ji snyed du 'byung bar 'gyur ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad bla na med pa'i mnyes par bya ba dang /sangs rgyas thams cad mchod cing /bsnyen bkur ba'i sems bskyed pas mnyes par bya'o snyam yang de la de
168a1(phal chen): @#//de ltar mi 'gyur snyam pa'i nem nur med/yid gnyis med the tsom med de/de ni gnas ma yin no//'di ni nem nur thams cad gcod pa'i sems bskyed pa gnyis pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan

168b8(phal chen): cad gcod pa'i sems bskyed pa dgu pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras gzhan yang byang chub sems/dpa' 'di snyam du sems te/'du shes kyi phyin ci log thams cad rab tu spangs pa'i sems skad cig gcig gi ye shes

200b3(phal chen): rnams sangs rgyas thams cad kyi chos kyi ye shes kyi gter bla na med pa 'thob pas sems can thams cad 'dul ba la brtson par byed pa dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu pa 'di dag ni byang chub sems dpa'
200b4(phal chen): rnamasakyi sdom pa ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad la mi 'khrug mi 'khrul pa'i sdom pa dang /sangs rgyas thams cad la bsam pa'i sdom pa dang /byang chub sems
200b5(phal chen): dpa' thams cad la bsam pa rlabs po che'i sdom pa dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen thams cad la bsam pa mi 'dor ba'i sdom pa dang /nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas yid la bya ba
200b6(phal chen): thams cad la yongasu mi rgyu ba'i sdom pa dang /byang cub sems dpa' chud za ba'i lam thams cad rnam par bzlog pa'i sdom pa dang /sems can thams cad la khong khro ba med pa'i sdom pa dang /
200b7(phal chen): dge ba'i rtsa ba brtsam pa thams cad mthar phyin par bya ba'i sdom pa dang /bdud thams cad rab tu pham par bya ba'i sdom pa dang/pha rol tu phyin pa thams cad yongasu rdzogs par bya ba'i sdom pa'o//
200b8(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sdom pa ste/de dag la gnas shing /byang chub sems dpa' rnal ye shes chen po'i sdom pa bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba
201a1(phal chen): @#//dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dbang ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar bskal pa brjod du med par tshe'i tshad byin gyis rlob pas tshe 
201a2(phal chen): la dbang ba dang /tinge 'dzin grangs med pa la nges par rtogs par gzhog cing /ye shes kyis 'jug pa'i tshul gyis sems la dbang pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad rgyan rnam par dbye ba du 
201a3(phal chen): mas rab tu brgyan par byin gyis brlab par ston pa'i tshul gyis yo byad la dbang ba dang /dus ji lta ba bzhin du las kyi rnam par smin pa byin gyis brlob pas las la dbang ba dang /'jig rten gyi 
201a4(phal chen): khams thams cad du skye ba ston pas skye ba la dbang ba dang /'jigs rten gyi khams thams cad sangs rgyas kyi yongasu gang bar lta bas mos pa la dbang ba dang /dus dang zhing gang du 'dod 
201a5(phal chen): par mngon par 'tshang rgya bas/smon lam la dbang ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul ba thams cad yongasu ston pa sa/rdzu 'phrul la dbang ba dang /chos kyi sgo dang /snang ba mtha' dang 
201a6(phal chen): dbus med pa yang dag par ston pas chos la dbang ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang /mi bsnyengs pa skad cig skad cig tu go bar yongasu ston pas/ye shes la dbang pa'o//kye rgyal 
201a7(phal chen): ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dbang ste/de dag la gnas shing /byang chub sems dpa' rnal sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byang chub kyi mnga'i pha rol tu phyin pa ye shes kyi rnam 
201a8(phal chen): par 'phrul ba bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi rnam par 'phrul ba rnams te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar sems can la 
201b1(phal chen): rnam par 'phrul ba dang /zhing la rnam par 'phrul ba dang /chos la rnam par 'phrul ba dang/lus la rnam par 'phrul ba dang /smon lam la rnam par 'phrul ba dang/spyod yul la rnam par 'phrul ba dang
201b2(phal chen): ye shes la rnam par 'phrul ba dag/mngon par shes pa la rnam par 'phrul ba dad/rdzu 'phrul la rnam par 'phrul ba /stobs la rnam par 'phrul bste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub
201b3(phal chen): sems dpa' rnams kyi rnam par 'phrul pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sems can la rnam par 'phrul ba gang zhe na/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems
201b4(phal chen): dpa' rnams kyi sems can rnam par 'phrul ba ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar sems can thams cad sems can med pas sgrol ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang sems can thams cad kyi 'du shes
201b5(phal chen): kyi rnam par byin gyis brlab pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang/sems can thams cad la dus la bab na chos ston pa rgyu na mi gcod pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang sems can gyi khams thaMd
201b6(phal chen): du sprul ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba dad/sems can gyi khams thams cad skra'i sbubs gcig tu btsud kyang gnod par mi 'gyur ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba /sems can gyi khams thams cid 'jig rten
201b7(phal chen): gyi khams byin gyis brlabs te/pha rol shes par byed pa dang /yongasu ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dag/sems can gyi khams tham sa cad brgya byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten gyi mgon po'i
201b8(phal chen): dbang yongasu ston ba'i rnam bar 'phrul ba dang /sems can gyi khams thams cad nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi gzugs dang /kha dog dang mtshan dang /cho ga dang /spyod lam yongasu ston pa'i
202a1(phal chen): @#//rnam par 'phrul ba dang /sems can gyi khams thams cad byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa thob pa yongasu ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /sems can gyi khams ma lus pa thaMd
202a2(phal chen): du thams cad mkhyen pa'i stobs mngon par rdzogs par sangs rgyas pa'i gzugs kyi sku dang /mtshan dang /dpe byad bzang po sna tshogs kyi rgyan yongasu ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras 
202a3(phal chen): bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sems can la rnam par 'phrul ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi zhing la rnam par 'phrul ba ste/
202a4(phal chen): bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar zhing thams cad zhing gcig tu byin gyis rlob pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /zhing thams cad skra'i sbubs gcig tu 'dzud pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba /zhing thaMd mi 
202a5(phal chen): zad pa la 'jug pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba /zhing thams cad dkyil mo krung gcig gis rgyas par 'gengs pa'i lus gcig yongasu ston pa'i rnam para'aphrul ba dang /zhing 
202a6(phal chen): thams cad rang gi lus su chud par yongasu ston pa'i rnam par'phrul ba dang /zhing thams cad rab tu bskyod kyang /sems can 'jigs par mi 'gyur ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /zhing thams
202a7(phal chen): cad kyi rgyan rnam par phye ba zhing gcig tu yongasu ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang/zhing gcig gis rnam par phye zhing thams cad kyi rgyan rnam par phye bar yongasu ston pa dang /zhing thams cad kyi de bzhin 
202a8(phal chen): gshegs pa'i 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku gcig gis yongasu khyab par sems can thams cad la ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /zhing phra ba dang /che ba dang /
202b1(phal chen): yangs pa dang /rgya che ba dang /zab pa dang snrel zhi dang /spyi'u tshugasu rgyu ba dang /gzhi mnyam pa la 'jug par yongasu gzhol bas phyogs kyi dra ba rnam par phye ba la 'jug par sems can ma lus pa thams cad
202b2(phal chen): la yongasu ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi zhing la rnam par 'phrul ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i
202b3(phal chen): rnams kyi chos la rnam par 'phrul ba ste /bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar chos thams cad chos gcig tu yang dag par gzhol bar bya ba dang /chos gcig po chos thams cad yang dag par gzhol ba byed kyang
202b4(phal chen): sems can gyi bsam ba dang /mi 'gal bar rnam par dmigs pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /chos thams cad shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pas sgrub par khong du chud par bya ba pha rol gyi go bar bya ba'i rnam
202b5(phal chen): par 'phrul ba dang /chos mthas cad 'du shes dang bral bar sems can mang pos khong du chud par bya ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang/chos thams cad la tshul gcig tu 'jug cing tshul mtha'
202b6(phal chen): yas par ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dag /chos thams cad la brjod med par 'jug cing sgo brgya stong gi pha rol gyi rgyud du bstan pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /chos thams cad sgo kun nas rgyu ba'i yi ge'i
202b7(phal chen): tshul la mkhas pa rnaMr 'phrul ba dang /chos thabs cad chos kyi sgo gcig dang mi 'gal ba la 'jug pa'i tshul khong du chud par bstan pas/bskal pa brjodu med cing mi zad pa rnam par 'phrul ba dang 
202b8(phal chen): chos thams cad sangs rgyas kyi chosu 'jug pa la sems can mang po gzud pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /chos thams cad mtha' dad dbus med par yongasu ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /chos thaMd
203a1(phal chen): la chags pa med pa'i mthar phyin cing mtha' dang dbus med pa'i sgyu ma'i dra ba rnam pa tha dad pa la 'jug pa med pas bskal pa mu med par mi zad par sems can la ston pa'i rnam 
203a2(phal chen): par 'phrul ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos la rnam par 'phrul ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po'adi dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi lus la rnam 
203a3(phal chen): par 'phrul ba ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar sems can thams cad kyi lus rang gi lus su byin gyi rlob cing /mos par byed pa la 'jug pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /rang gi lus sems can thams cad
203a4(phal chen): kyi lus kyi 'du shes su byin gyi rlob pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dad/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi sku sangs rgyas gcig gi skur mos shing /'jug pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa dang /sangs rgyas gcig gi sku 
203a5(phal chen): sangs rgyas ma lus pa thams cad kyi skur 'jug cing /gzhan la yongs su ston pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dag zhing thams cad du nam mkha' byin gyis rlob pa'i rnam par 'phrulab dang /
203a6(phal chen): dus gsum thams cad chos kyi sku gcig gis yongs su rgyas par sems can la ston pa'i rnam par 'phrulab dag /lus gcig tu ting nge 'dzin la mnyam par bzhag cing lus mtha' yas pa las 
203a7(phal chen): rnam par ldang ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang/lus gcig gis mngon par sangs rgyas shing sems can snyed kyi lus su yongs su ston pa'i rnam bar 'phrul ba dang /sems can thams cad kyi lus seMn 
203a8(phal chen): gcig gi lus su ston pa'i rnam par 'khrul ba dang /sems can tha mas cad kyi lus chos kyi skur ston cing /chos kyi sku sems can gyi lus su ston pa'i rnam pa 'phrul ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i 
203b1(phal chen): sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lus la rnam par 'phrul ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi smon lam la rnam par 'phrul ba ste bcu gang zhe na/
203b2(phal chen): 'di ltar byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi smon lam rang gi smon lam du byin gyis rlob pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyi smon lam gyi stobs dang mthu mngon 
203b3(phal chen): par rdzogs par byang chub bar yongasu ston rnar 'phrul ba dang /sems can ji ltar smin pa rang rang gi bsam ba bzhin du bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar bya 
203b4(phal chen): ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang/bskal pa 'grangs pa mu med pa thams cad du smon lam gyi rgyun mi gcod pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /rnam par shes pa'i lus las gyen du bskyod de/ye shes kyi 
203b5(phal chen): lus la'ang mi gnas pa'i smon lam la dbang bsgyur bas lus thams cad yongasu ston ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba dangrang gi lus tshad gyur pas pha rol gyi tshogs kyi smon lam thams cad 
203b6(phal chen): yongasu rdzogs par bya ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba de/sems can thams cad yongasu smin par bya ba'i smon lam las phyir mi ldog pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dang /bskal pa 'grangs pa thams cad byang chub 
203b7(phal chen): kyi spyod pas rgyas par dgang pa'i smon lam rgyun mi 'chad pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba dad/skra'i sbubs gcig tu mngon par 'tshang rgya ba'i smon lam gyi stobs kyis sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad du 
203b8(phal chen): rgyas par dgang zhing /'jig rten gyi khams brjod med pa'i yang brjod med pa/sems can gcig tu ston pa'i smon lam la rnam par 'phrul ba dang/chos kyi tshig gcig brjod pas chos kyi dbyings 
204a1(phal chen): ma lus pa thams cad rgyas par bkang ste/chos kyi sprin chen po byung ba las chos bdud rtsi'i char rab tu 'bab cing /rig pa dang rnam par grol ba'i glog slang ba dang /yang dag 
204a2(phal chen): pa de kho na nyid kyi chos kyi 'brug sgra mngon par 'byung bas sems can gyi khams thams cad yongs su ngoms par bya ba'i smon lam chen po yongs su rdzogs par bya bas rnam par rtse ba'i rnam par 'phrul ba ste/kye rgyal 
204a3(phal chen): ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi smon lam la rnam par 'phrul pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi spyod yul la rnam par 'phrul ba 
204a4(phal chen): ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' rnam ni chos kyi dbyings kyi spyod yul dang yang ldan la sems can gyi khams kyi spyod yul du'ang snang ngo//kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub
204a5(phal chen): sems dpa' rnams ni sangs rgyas kyi spyod yul la mngon par dga' ba'ang yin la/bdud kyi spyod yul la gnas par yang snang ngo//kye rgyal ba la sras byang chub sems dpa'i rnams ni/mya 
204a6(phal chen): ngan las 'das pa'i spyod yul dang yang ldan 'khor ba'i spyod yul las kyang mi g.yo'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras byangs chub sems dpa' rnams ni thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi spyod yul dang ldan la 
204a7(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i spyod yul gyi rgyud kyang rgyun mi gtsong ngo//kye rgyal ba'i srasabyang chub sems dpa' rnams na zhi ba dang /rab tu zhi ba dang /nye bar zhi ba'i spyod yul dang yang ldan/sems can ma 
204a8(phal chen): zhi ba rab tu ma zhi ba nye bar ma zhi ba'i spyod yul can rnams kyang rnam par mi spong ngo//kye rgyal ba'i sras/byang chub sems dpa' rnam ni blang ba med pa dang /dor ba med pa dang /spro ba med pa dang /mi 'phro 
204b1(phal chen): ba dang /mtshan ma med pa dang /dngos po med pa dang /rnam par mi dmigs pa dang /bar snang dang mnyam pa'i spyod yul dang yang ldan la/sems can spros pa dang /mtshan ma thams cad du dmigs pa'i spyod 
204b2(phal chen): yul can rnams kyi gnas na yang snang ngo //kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni stobs dang /rnam par thar pa dang /rtogs pa dang /ye shes dben pa'i spyod yul dang yang lda la/'jig rten gyi khams 
204b3(phal chen): rnam par mi spong ngo //kye rgyal ba'i sris byang chub sems dpa' rnas ni bsam gtan dang /rnam par thar ba dang /mngon par 'tshang rgya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi rgyan dang /spyod yul 
204b4(phal chen): thams cad skye bar yang snang ngo //kye rgya ba'i sras byang chub sems/dpa' rnams ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i spyod pa dang /mngon par sras bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnam kyi 
204b5(phal chen): thams cad kyang thob la/nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad kyi spyod lam dang /cho ga yang yongs su ston te/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni byang chub sems dpa' rnamad kyi 
204b6(phal chen): spyod yul la rnam par 'phrul ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po' di dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi ye shes la rnam par 'phrul ba ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar tha dad pa yang dag par 
204b7(phal chen): shes pa'i ye shes mi zad pa la rnam par 'phrul ba dang /rmongs pa med pa'i gzungs thams cad thob pa'i ye shes la rnam par 'phrulab dang /smon lam shin tu rnam par gdon mi za ba bstan pa dang /
204b8(phal chen): sems can gyi khams thams cad kyi dbang po yongs su 'dzin pa dang /khong du chud pa'i ye shes la rnam par ma 'phrul ba dang /rang gi sems kyi ye shes pha rol gyi sems kyi ye shes dang sems gcig 
205a1(phal chen): @#//las sems can thams cad kyi spyod pa dang /rnam par g.yo bar byed pa'i ye shes la rnam par 'phrul ba dang /sems can gyi khams thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /bag la 
205a2(phal chen): nyal ba dang /bag chags dang /nyon mongs pa'i nad la gang dang 'byor pa'i sman yang dag par gtong ba'i ye shes rnam par 'phrul pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu la skad cig gcig gis 
205a3(phal chen): 'jug pa'i ye shes la rnam par sprul ba dang /dus gsum gyi bskal pa'i grangs thams cad la chags pa med pa'i ye shes yangs pa sems can gzhan gyis thob par byed pa'i ye shes la rnams par 
205a4(phal chen): 'phrul ba dang /thogs pa med pa'i sems kyi skad cig tu mngon par 'tshang rgya bar 'gro ba thams cad du yongs su ston pa'i ye shes la rnam par 'phrul ba dang /sems cad gcig gi 'du shes kyis/
205a5(phal chen): sems can ma lus pa thams cad kyi las dang bya ba la mngon sum du ye shes la rnam par 'phrulab dang /sems can gcig gi sgra dbyangs kyis sgra thams cad la 'jug pa/yongs su ston

205a7(phal chen): dpa' rnams kyi mngon par shes pa la rnam par 'phrul ba ste bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad lus yongs su ston kyang /lus gcig tu dmigs pa'i mngon par shes pa la rnal 
205a8(phal chen): bar 'phrul ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa gcig gi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor du de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor dang /chos bstan pa yongs su 'dzin pa'i mngon par shes

205b4(phal chen): ston cing /sems can gyi go bar byed pa'i mngon pa shes pa la rnam par 'phrulab dang /'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad brgyan par bya ba'i mngon par shes pa la/rnam par 'phrul ba
205b5(phal chen): dang /dus thams cad mnyam pa nyidu 'jug pa'i mngon par shes pa rnam par 'phrul ba dang /sangs rgyas thams cad kyi byang chub tu spyod pa'i smon lam yang dag par bskyed pa dang /chos kyi
205b6(phal chen): snang ba rab tu dgye ba'i mngon par shes pa rnams par 'phrul ba dang /lha dang /klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /dri za dang /lha ma yin dang /nam mkha' lding dang /mi 'am ci dang /lto 'phye chen po dang /brgya
205b7(phal chen): byin dang /tshangs pa dang /'jig rten gyi mgon po thams cad kyis mngon par shes pa nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems dpa' dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu dang /
205b8(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba thams cad kyis yongasu bsrung ba'i mngon par shes pa dang rnam par 'phrul ba dang kye rgyal ba'i sras mdor na/byang chub sems dpa' ni mngon par
206a1(phal chen): @#//shes pas chos thams cad la yang dag par gzhol ba yin te/bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi mngon par shes pa la rnam par 'phrul ba'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po 'di

211b2(phal chen): pas dkar po'i chos yongs su 'dus pa yin/mngon par shes pa dang /bsam pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs pa yin/rgyun chad pa med par sangs rgyas kyi byang chub la rab tu rten pa yin/byang chub sems dpa'i 
211b3(phal chen): spyod pa thams cad yongs su rjogs pa yin/sangs rgyas thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes dang /rdzu 'phrul la mngon par dbang bskur ba yang sems can yongs su smin par bya ba'i phyir/spyod pa nye bar blang ba de yang 
211b4(phal chen): rgyun mi gcod de/de dag 'di snyam du bdag cag gis/sems can yang yongs su smin par ma gyur la/bdag cag kyang sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog gi mthu phyung ba ston par mi nus pa 'am gcig tu mi 'gyur

211b6(phal chen): sems can yongs su sminapa'i tshul ji lta ba bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi yul yongs su ston te/byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa yang rgyun mi gcod la/byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam chen pos skye ba yang rgyun mi

214b2(phal chen): bsam pa'i go cha shin tu bgos pas de dag ji ltar nyon mongs shing /byis pa'i skye bo'i 'du 'dzi dang ji ltar brten tam/nyan thos dang rang sangs rgyas kyi tshogs kyi nang du ji ltar phyin kyang rung ste/thams cad mkhyen pa'i sems

214b5(phal chen): 'dzis ji ltar rten kyang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ses rin po che 'od gsal zhing /snang ba bzang po de chud zos par mi byed de/de dag kyang nyon mongs pa'i rnyog pa de las zlog cing /thams cad

214b8(phal chen): dpa'i chos ma 'dres pa dgu pa'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras/byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni rang gi yul gyi ye shes kyi chos la dbang bsgyur ba'i pha rol tu phyin pa dam pa thob pa ste/rang byung gi ye shes kyi dbang bskur bas

215a2(phal chen): sum tshogs pa dang /'gro ba thams cad du dge ba'i bshes gnyen brten pa las phyir mi ldog cing /de bzhin gshegs pa rim gror bya ba rgyun mi gcod pa de ni byang chub sems dpa'i chos ma 'dres pa ste/'di ni

216b1(phal chen): len kyang yongasu mi skyo bas/snying rje chen po'i rang bzhin dang /'gro ba thams cad yongasu skyob pas/byams pa chen po'i rang bzhin dang/'gro ba thams cad nye bar 'tsho bas bsod nams kyi rang bzhin dang /sangs rgyas thaMd kyi

248b5(phal chen): pa'i phyir brjod du med pa'i don dang /dus gsum mnyam pa nyid kyi phyir/yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin gyi don dang /ro mnyam pa nyid kyi phyir chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa'i don dang /de bzhin 
248b6(phal chen): nyid kyi rjesu 'brang ba'i phyir de bzhin nyid kyi don dang /yang dag pa'i mtha' rtogs pa'i phyir yang dag pa'i mtha'i don dang /sdug bsngal gyi phung po yang dag par gcad cing /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod 
248b7(phal chen): pa bsgrub pa bskyed pa'i phyir/mya ngan las 'das pa chen po'i don dang /kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag ni/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi don te/de dag la gnas shing /byang chub sems dpa' rnalas thams cad 
248b8(phal chen): mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi don bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu bo 'di dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi chos te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar ji skad du smras pa de bzhin 
249a1(phal chen): @#//du bya ba'i phyir/bden pa'i chos dang /len cing 'dzin pa thams cad yongs su gcad pa'i phyir yongs su 'dzin pa med pa'i chos dang /nyon mongs pa'i 'bar ba thams cad rab tu 
249a2(phal chen): zhi bar bya ba'i phyir/rtsod pa med pa'i chos dang /yongs su gdung ba dang bral ba'i phyir zhi ba'i chos dang /'dod chags kyi dri ma dang nyon mongs pa thams cad dang bral ba'i phyir/'dod chags dang bral ba'i chos dang /

249a4(phal chen): dang /rang bzhin gyis kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa'i phyir/rang bzhin gyi chos dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bsgrub cing yongs su gzung ba'i phyir/phung po thams cad spangs te/mya ngan las 'das

249a6(phal chen): nyid bla na med pa 'thob par 'gyur ba dag go/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu'o 'di dag ni/byang chub sems pa'i rnams kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs te/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar dkon mchog gsum gyi gdung rgyun mi 
249a7(phal chen): gcod pa'i phyir/pha rol byang chub kyi sems la yid dam 'char gzhug pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs dang /mi dge ba'i chos thams cad spang zhing dge ba'i chos thams cad yang dag par bsdu bar bya ba'i phyir/
249a8(phal chen): yongs su bsngo ba'i rnam pa bcu'i rjes su 'brang ba'i bsod nams kyi tshogs dag 'jig rten gsum gyi bsod nams zil gyis gnan pa'i phyir/shes rab dang ye shes bstan pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs/dang sems 
249b1(phal chen): can gyi khams thams cad pha rol gyi mthar bsgral ba'i phyir/yongs su skyo ba med pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs dang /yongs su gzung ba thams cad gtong ba'i phyir/nang dang phyi'i dngos po thams cad yongs su gtong ba'i 
249b2(phal chen): bsod nams kyi tshogs dang/brtson 'grus chen po brtsams pas sems kyi sgo gtan 'byed cing mchod sbyin rab tu bya ba'i phyir/mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po yongs su rdzogs par bya ba'i bsod nams kyi tshogs dad/thabs 
249b3(phal chen): dang tshul dang 'thad pas yongs su bzung ba'i phyir/dge ba'i rtsa ba rab 'bring dang /tha mas rlom sems med par yongs su bsngo ba'i bsod nams kyi tshogasadang /skyes bu dam pa'i rnam par gnon pa yongs su bstan pa'i phyir/
